# Changelog

## Unreleased

- **Saying 'new topic' starts a fresh session; a normal @mention keeps the open one** — SESSION-3.b (in flight).
- **Each model gets its own window; at about 80% a model writes the summary and the task stays pinned word for word** — SESSION-5 / SESSION-5.a (in flight).

## 0.0.46

### Daemon / Discord

- **With only the daemon running, a scheduled run's question still reaches me by DM** — [#411](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/411) (AUTONOMOUS-7.a; tip orphan [#425](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/425)): `corvidinho daemon` DMs the owner each pending schedule ask over Discord's REST API with the bot token while no bridge runs; `daemon.started` carries `ownerDm`; a live bridge on the same data dir still owns channel posts and never double-DMs the question.

### Agent

- **A headless agent CLI can be one of my models, in my own runs only, with the same tools inside the talk's worktree** — [#413](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/413) (AGENT-13 / AGENT-13.a; tip orphan [#426](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/426)): configure an OpenAI-compatible / Ollama / Anthropic / headless-agent-CLI model with no built-in default; a headless agent CLI model runs only in the owner's own runs, with the same tools as other models, inside that talk's own worktree; other runs skip it and use the next model; after a stall move the weak model is dropped from authors so it can second-model review (GITHUB-9.a authorship already on tip).

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.46** — restart the Discord bridge and `github watch` after update so presence (DISCORD-12) reports 0.0.46. The bridge already ticks schedules; do **not** start a second `corvidinho daemon`. Source `~/.config/corvidinho/env` **and** `watch.env` so `GIPHY_API_KEY` and the watch username stay set. Leave Brave off (do not invent keys). No schema bump; NDJSON protocol stays 2. v0.0.46 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main.
- Needs operator action: (1) Update live checkout to the **release commit** (not a tip-orphan ahead of it). (2) Restart bridge + github watch via pidfile pattern with env + watch.env loaded. (3) Post a feature/version summary in the allowlisted main channel.
- Rollback to the v0.0.45 tag: schema unchanged. That tag does not include AUTONOMOUS-7.a daemon schedule DM or AGENT-13/13.a headless agent CLI model.
- Parked: #394 stays closed; COS HI capture and COMMUNITY-1..4 stay parked; drafts #405/#393 remain HI-backed DRAFT (not rebased this wave).

## 0.0.44


### Admin / Discord / Watch

- **As owner I can change deny lists and the GitHub repo allow lists with `/admin`, every change audited** — [#404](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/404) (ADMIN-3.c part 1; tip orphan [#418](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/418)): `/admin deny add|remove` (channel, user, role, github_org, github_repo, github_user) and `/admin github add|remove` (org, repo); owner re-check; env-only and lockout refusals; SAFE-5 audit; live allowlist splice; `github watch` re-reads the allowlist every poll (REQ-watch-043). `[github].users` stays file/env. Part 2 mutes (#407) still draft.

### Safe / plugins

- **The shell and runners refuse raw-SQL wipes and overwrites of Corvidinho's own store** — [#396](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/396) (SAFE-4; tip orphan [#418](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/418)): only `memory-forget` / `memory-override` (SAFE-18.a DM card + one-time code) may change memories; shell-exec and language runners refuse store targets fail-closed.
- **My own memory forget and override by id ask me on a DM card with Approve and a one-time code, and an override shows the new text** — [#409](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/409) (SAFE-18.a; tip orphan [#416](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/416)).

### Agent / autonomy / personas / COS

- **`shell-exec` refuses SpecSync lifecycle steps written as globs or braces, or reached through an expanding or xargs-fed subcommand** — [#403](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/403) (AGENT-18.a follow-up to #372; tip orphan [#417](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/417)).
- **Named personas are their own files with model and skill tags; run as one and a lead picks one by skill** — [#410](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/410) (AUTONOMOUS-2.a / 5.a; tip orphan [#416](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/416)).
- **Every working day each of us gets a short briefing DM about our own work, in our own hours and timezone** — [#412](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/412) (COS briefing behavior; tip orphan [#416](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/416)). COS-* HI capture into `hi/cos.md` remains parked pending Leif confirm.
- **Once a session question's buttons expire the session stops waiting; schedule questions still wait** — [#408](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/408) (AUTONOMY-6.b; tip orphan [#415](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/415)).

### Watch / SpecSync follow-ups

- **Edited text never gets its author's role; SAFE-13 scans what the owner didn't write; WATCH never writes its checkout; audit names the GitHub trigger** — [#398](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/398) (IDENTITY-12.a follow-up; tip orphan [#415](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/415)).
- **The verify lane's `spec-check` step also starts without my cloud credentials** — [#402](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/402) (SAFE-21.b follow-up; tip orphan [#414](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/414)).
- **#375's archived review records the post-merge review that ran** — [#406](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/406) (AUTONOMY-10.b follow-up; tip orphan [#415](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/415)).

## 0.0.42

### Agent / land-wave

- **In a Trust repo the verify gate also runs `fledge trust verify`** — [#364](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/364) (AGENT-18 Trust clause; tip orphan [#376](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/376)): with a `.trust.toml` in the session base, HEAD or the working tree, verify runs `fledge trust verify` after `fledge lanes run verify` and both must pass; a fledge with no `trust` command fails closed with the exact reason. Corvidinho's own repo still has no Trust re-add.
- **`/work` runs its second-model review rounds before the PR and skips with `not-reviewed` otherwise** — [#365](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/365) (GITHUB-9; tip orphan [#377](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/377)): completes the `/work` half of the second-model review gate (#341 already did `github-pr-create`). With no second model there is no PR and the reply says why.
- **Where a repo uses hi it drafts criteria and asks on a card before capturing them** — [#367](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/367) (AGENT-18 hi drafts; tip orphan [#381](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/381)): drafts proposed criteria, raises an Approve card, and only captures after the owner OK — never invents HI.
- **In a hi repo it never changes the criteria itself** — [#348](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/348) (AGENT-18 hi guard; tip orphan [#366](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/366)): any `hi/` change no approved capture made blocks done and the PR.
- **In a non-git project my runs work in the folder itself** — [#351](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/351) (AGENT-1.a/1.b/1.c; tip orphan [#378](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/378)): owner runs work in the project folder; file tools leave root `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md` alone; schedules keep their own folder; others only read there.
- **After the one nudge it moves to the next stronger model in the order I set, and says so** — [#370](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/370) (AGENT-17, AGENT-17.a; tip orphan [#384](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/384)): completes the escalate half of AGENT-17 via optional `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_ORDER`; with no order set it does not move and the one nudge still happens.
- **`shell-exec` refuses SpecSync change approve/review/finalize/ship** — [#372](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/372) (AGENT-18.a; tip orphan [#386](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/386)): only a human, or Corvidinho's own green-lane settle, does those lifecycle steps.

### Discord / autonomy

- **I or a schedule's creator can stop a scheduled run in progress from Discord** — [#369](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/369) (AGENT-3.c; tip orphan [#383](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/383)): same Stop button / stop words as a chat run.
- **Before each call it counts a worst-case reply toward the cap** — [#371](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/371) (AUTONOMY-8.a; tip orphan [#385](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/385)): so long replies cannot pass the spend cap unasked; replies are never cut short.
- **The fixed bridge-live note is system text and posts without the owner's OK** — [#375](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/375) (AUTONOMY-10.b; tip orphan [#389](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/389)): docs/criterion capture; runtime already posted without must-ask (DISCORD-ANNOUNCE-4 unchanged).

### CLI / safe / watch / plugins

- **Doctor and init say when the verify lane runs no test step Corvidinho can read** — [#368](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/368) (CLI-4; tip orphan [#382](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/382)): one `[warn] test-step` line when no recognised test runner is in the verify lane.
- **The verify lane, shell and runners start without my cloud credentials** — [#373](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/373) (SAFE-21.b; tip orphan [#387](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/387)): drops KUBECONFIG / AWS / Google Cloud / Azure and similar so they cannot reach prod by accident.
- **On GitHub, the owner and team I've declared get their role's tools behind the must-ask gate; strangers stay community** — [#374](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/374) (IDENTITY-12.a; tip orphan [#388](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/388)).
- **`/work`, `/schedule` and the scheduler can be turned off in `[corvidinho.plugins]`, and existing installs stay on** — [#350](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/350) (PLUGIN-5/5.a; tip orphan [#390](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/390)).

### Ops

The bridge already ticks schedules; do **not** start a second `corvidinho daemon`. Source `~/.config/corvidinho/env` **and** `watch.env` so `GIPHY_API_KEY` and the watch username stay set. No schema bump; NDJSON protocol stays 2. Live dogfood still needs `GIPHY_API_KEY`. v0.0.42 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main.

(4) Optional: set `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_ORDER` for AGENT-17.a escalate; toggle `[corvidinho.plugins]` work/schedule if you want them off.

## 0.0.41

### Agent

- **When an ask names a plugin, or asks for a GIF, that this run cannot offer, it soft-lands with the real gap** — [#360](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/360) (REQ-agent-742): instead of inventing a tool or asking a vague "what do you mean by install?" clarify, the reply names the concrete gap. **not installed** — not registered, or not in this project's Fledge plugin list when discovery ran. **not allowlisted** — dangerous tool, SAFE-1. **not configured** — the tool's real key (`GIPHY_API_KEY` or `BRAVE_SEARCH_API_KEY`). **role** — community stays read/chat (ROLES-CHAT-2 / PLUGIN-9); Fledge commands stay owner-only. **tier** — below the run's capability tier. An HI id or an open PR number is cited only when a lookup actually returned it (`hi/*.md`, and `gh pr list` on this repo). Tenor, and any other provider that is not an offered tool, is not invented. If `fledge-gif` or `gif-search` is already offered, the model still runs (prefer `fledge-gif`). A vague install `ask-human` is replaced by the gap or steered back to the offered tool. No new HI id (AGENT-18 still needs a human confirm of new criteria). No schema bump. SpecSync tip orphan after the squash is archived in [#361](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/361).

### Already on the previous tip

- **Prefer `fledge-gif` over native `gif-search`** — [#358](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/358) (tip orphan [#359](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/359)) is already on the previous tip, the main commit this bump sits on, not new behaviour introduced by the version number. Live dogfood still needs `GIPHY_API_KEY` in the bridge env. This release does not inject a key, does not add `discord-send-file` or `discord-post-message` to the allowlist, and does not remove `gif-search`. `fledge-gif` stays the preferred GIF path when it is offered; native `gif-search` stays the secondary GIPHY path from 0.0.40.

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.41** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update so presence (DISCORD-12) reports 0.0.41. No schema bump; no new slash command; NDJSON protocol stays 2. No new allowlist names. v0.0.41 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main.
- Needs operator action: (1) Restart bridge, daemon and `github watch`. (2) GIF dogfood still needs `GIPHY_API_KEY` (and `fledge-gif` allowlisted at code tier, which the live allowlist already includes along with `gif-search` and `web-search`). (3) Do not add `discord-send-file` or `discord-post-message` for this cut. Parked drafts #348, #350 and #351 are not in 0.0.41.

That tag does not include the missing-plugin soft-land (#360) or the fledge-gif prefer path (#358), both of which landed on main after the 0.0.40 package bump. ## 0.0.40

### Plugins

- **It can search the web through Brave when I set a key, for me and the team only** — [#326](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/326) (PLUGIN-7, PLUGIN-9): `web-search` in `plugins/web` via Brave Search API (`BRAVE_SEARCH_API_KEY`). Dangerous, minTier 1, SAFE-1 allowlist, owner and declared team only (`TEAM_SEARCH_TOOLS`). Results fenced + SAFE-13 scanned. Each search reserves ~$0.005 against SAFE-8. Replies that got an answer end with `Search by Brave` once.
- **It can find a GIF through GIPHY, with the safety filter at medium, and post it as a link when asked, for me and the team only** — [#331](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/331) (PLUGIN-8, PLUGIN-9): `gif-search` in `plugins/gif` via GIPHY (`GIPHY_API_KEY`, `contentfilter=medium`). Link-only posting (no download/attach). Team shares the tool with web-search. Spend recorded at $0. Summary carries `Powered By GIPHY`. SpecSync tip orphans after squash archived in [#354](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/354) / [#355](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/355).

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.40** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update. Allowlist `web-search` and `gif-search` in `CORVIDINHO_ALLOWLIST` and set `BRAVE_SEARCH_API_KEY` / `GIPHY_API_KEY` for live dogfood (PLUGIN-9 docs E.3 / E.3.b). No schema bump; NDJSON protocol stays 2.
- Needs operator action: (1) Restart bridge/daemon/watch. (2) Add `web-search,gif-search` to `CORVIDINHO_ALLOWLIST`. (3) Set API keys. (4) Team members already get both tools when allowlisted (IDENTITY-10).
- Rollback to 0.0.39: schema unchanged. 0.0.39 has neither tool; drop the new allowlist names and keys.

## 0.0.39

### Real dev teammate (M3)

- **Before every PR opens, a second model reviews the diff in bounded rounds, and the PR body lists what it raised and what changed** — [#341](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/341) (GITHUB-9, GITHUB-9.a; REQ-plugins / work review gate): `github-pr-create` now runs a second-model review gate after the repo gate. The reviewer is the first other configured model (GITHUB-9.a). Bounded rounds; the PR lists findings and what changed. `/work` that is held for review gets reason `not-reviewed` and keeps the branch pushed. Reviewer tokens ride `usageByModel` for the owner's DISCORD-15.a spend footer.
- **An idle timeout and a turn cap I set stop stalled or endless runs, and it says so** — [#342](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/342) (AGENT-12; REQ-agent-244, REQ-agent-312, REQ-cli-125, REQ-discord-125, REQ-watch-125, REQ-plugins-125): optional `CORVIDINHO_MAX_TURNS` caps model/tool rounds per execute attempt (default stays 8). Optional `CORVIDINHO_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 600000) runs a per-run idle watchdog in `src/agent/limits.ts`. Soft-land stop reasons name idle vs turn-cap; workers and schedules honour the same limits. Docs: `--help`, `.env.example`, DISCORD-GO-LIVE E.10, discord/WATCH/DAEMON.
- **My local CLI `task run` may use the allowlisted shell, language runners and Fledge lane/task runs inside the worktree it made for itself** — [#346](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/346) (SAFE-3.a CLI half; REQ-cli-681, REQ-agent-503): completes SAFE-3.a for local CLI. `taskRun` passes the worktree top as `talkWorktree` only when it made its own worktree and there is no role session; `shellToolsGate` grants allowlisted `SAFE3A_TOOLS` there. `--here`, non-git folders and spawned children stay refused. Must-ask (#319) still applies. In-place local runs keep the prior SAFE-3.a refusal line.

### Talk anywhere (M2)

- **Its first 20 replies in a public thread each wait for my OK on an Approve card** — [#347](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/347) (AUTONOMY-10, AUTONOMY-10.a): public threads (`PublicThread`, `AnnouncementThread`, forum/media posts) hold each of the first 20 replies for the owner's must-ask Approve card — even dictated text and replies to the owner. Clarify asks go pending only after they are posted. `discord-send-file` gets `CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_REPLY_PUBLIC_THREAD` while replies still wait. Stop button, failed-run lines and spend paths do not wait.

### Fixes

- **A failed GitHub (WATCH) run's comment says the model call failed in one plain line, never the provider's raw error body** — [#343](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/343): spawn client returns `failureReason` / `stderrTail`; summary uses `watchPublicFailureLine` (host stripped). Aligns WATCH with Discord's DISCORD-3.b reason line from 0.0.38.
- **A failed delegate or council worker hands the lead model one plain failure line, never the model provider's raw error body** — [#349](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/349) (REQ-agent-117, REQ-agent-118, REQ-watch-009): `workerFailureLine` builds harness text only (timeout / interrupt, result error without provider host via shared `withoutProviderHost`, protocol-mismatch notice, no-provider notice, or `the worker failed (exit N)` / spawn scrub). Lead tool results and council voice transcript entries never carry provider bodies, org names or request ids from a failed worker.
- **The DISCORD-3.b 401 check masks the run's own random ids, so an id holding `401` no longer fails the test** — [#345](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/345) (REQ-discord-032): test-only flake fix in `tests/discord.failed-reply.test.ts`.

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.39** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update so presence (DISCORD-12) and spawners pick up the new version and the AGENT-12 / SAFE-3.a / AUTONOMY-10 / GITHUB-9 / plain-failure behaviour. No schema bump; no new slash command; NDJSON protocol stays 2. New optional env: `CORVIDINHO_MAX_TURNS`, `CORVIDINHO_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` (#342). Internal stamp `CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_REPLY_PUBLIC_THREAD` is set by the bridge for `discord-send-file` only (#347) — do not export it. v0.0.39 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main.
- Needs operator action: (1) Restart bridge, daemon and `github watch`. (2) Optionally set `CORVIDINHO_MAX_TURNS` / `CORVIDINHO_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` if the defaults (8 turns / 10 min idle) are wrong for your box. (3) Local CLI shell (#346): only runs that made their own worktree get `SAFE3A_TOOLS` when allowlisted; `--here` still has no shell.
- Rollback to 0.0.38: schema unchanged. 0.0.38 ignores the new env knobs and the public-thread reply gate; failed workers / WATCH comments may again surface provider bodies; local CLI has no SAFE-3.a shell; PRs open without the second-model review gate.

## 0.0.38

### Talk anywhere (M2)

- **Every Discord run's progress message now carries a red Stop button that the person who asked, or the owner, presses to stop the run — completing AGENT-3.a for chat, ask picks and Answer forms, `/session start` and `/work`** — [#337](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/337) (AGENT-3.a, AGENT-3.b, AGENT-3, DISCORD-DENY-3, DISCORD-15.a, SAFE-20; REQ-discord-303, REQ-discord-302, REQ-discord-548): in 0.0.37 (#332) a run could only be stopped by sending `stop` or `cancel` as the whole message. While a run goes, its progress message — the chat `Working on your request...` message, the Choose or Answer stub a pick or a private answer resumed in (the Stop button takes that button's place while the run goes; the answer's own buttons come back with the answer), or the `/session start` / `/work` progress message — now shows one red (danger-style) **Stop** button with custom id `cvstop:<runId>`. Working edits leave it alone; it is cleared when the run is done, fails (a run that throws included) or is stopped. A press has its own `cvstop` branch in the bridge's `onComponent`, apart from the Approve cards and the asks, and passes the same gates as an ask press, now one shared `pressPassesGates`: the press channel and the talk's own channel must be allowlisted (else the admin tip or the zero-width ack), then the actor gate (the zero-width ack for a deny-listed user) and the mute / rate gate (the usual muted or rate-limited reply), every refusal ephemeral; a forged form submit on the button is ignored. It then counts only on the progress message of the run that is going now, in that channel and with that run id — run ids restart at `run_1` in every bridge process — so a button left from a finished run or from before a restart answers a private `Nothing is running.` and stops nothing (a stale button in a thread whose session is no longer known gets the channel gate's zero-width ack (for an admin or the owner, the allowlist tip) instead). The requester or the owner stops the run through the same `SessionRunControl.stop` as the stop words: the run's whole process tree is killed, the presser gets a private `⏹ Stopping the run.`, the progress message becomes `⏹ Stopped` with its footer (model and time; tokens and cost only on the owner's own runs), an Approve card the run was waiting on closes as a no, `[discord] run <run id> of session <session id> stopped by <user id>` is logged, and the messages waiting behind it still run afterwards, in order, each with its own button (AGENT-3.b). Anyone else gets a private `This Stop button isn't for you.` and the run goes on. A second press, or a `stop` reply after a press, aborts nothing more. The restart's interrupted notice (REQ-discord-311) now also clears the button on a chat, pick or Answer progress message that a dead process left. The stop words still answer publicly; the button's ack is private and the `⏹ Stopped` progress message is the public sign. No env var, config key, slash command, table or schema change. Not built: stopping a schedule's run from Discord (Leif, round 13: the owner or the schedule's creator; a later PR), so schedule runs carry no button. Known limits: when the bridge itself stops, a `/session start` or `/work` progress message (they keep no in-flight row) keeps its button for good (a press does not clear it), and every press answers `Nothing is running.`; a press in the short window after the agent exited still gets `⏹ Stopping the run.` but the full answer is posted, and once `/work`'s PR step has started the PR still opens (as with the stop words); a press goes through the mute / rate gate and counts toward the presser's rate limit, so a muted requester cannot press Stop, nor can a rate-limited one until the window passes (the owner still can).
- **A local `corvidinho task run` in a git repo now works in its own new worktree made from HEAD and cleans it up at the end; `--here` runs it in the current checkout as before, and every product surface passes `--here`** — [#338](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/338) (SESSION-WORKTREE-1.a, SESSION-WORKTREE-1, AGENT-1.a, SAFE-3.a; REQ-cli-122, REQ-discord-014, REQ-discord-073, REQ-watch-006, REQ-watch-073, REQ-agent-117, REQ-agent-503, REQ-plugins-117, REQ-plugins-118): `task run` always worked in, and edited, the checkout it was started in. Now (new `src/worktree/cli-run.ts`), in a git repo it makes a linked worktree from `HEAD` with the same `ensureTalkWorkspace` Discord talks use, from the realpath repo top (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel`): under `WORKTREE_BASE_DIR`, else `.corvid-worktrees` next to the repo, named `talk-cli_<uuid prefix>-<digest>` on branch `talk/cli_…`, and the run works in the same subdirectory it was started from. Nothing is copied into it (uncommitted and untracked files, a local `.env`, `node_modules`) and nothing is installed there, though the process keeps the environment it started with (a `.env` Bun loaded from the start directory still applies); the run's first event says `Working in a new worktree <dir> (branch <branch>) made from HEAD: uncommitted and untracked files in this checkout are not included and nothing is installed there. Pass --here to run in this checkout.` (stderr in text mode, a `Text` event with `--json` / ndjson). `--here` is read only from `task run`'s own arguments before the first `--` — never from `--task` text, and it is no global flag; `corvidinho --help` and the usage line show `[--here]`. A run always stays in place in a directory that is not in a git repo (AGENT-1.a) and in a child a product surface spawned — a role session (`CORVIDINHO_ACTING_IS_ADMIN` present: chat, slash, schedules), a WATCH (`CORVIDINHO_WATCH_SESSION_ID`) or Discord (`CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_SESSION_ID`) session, or a `delegate` / `council` worker (`CORVIDINHO_DELEGATE_DEPTH` above 0) — so those never nest a worktree, even under a spawner that does not pass `--here`; the Discord spawn client (the bridge and `corvidinho daemon`), the WATCH spawn client and `buildDelegateSpawn` (delegate workers and council voices) now all pass `--here`. When the run ends (done, failed, blocked, cancelled or thrown) the worktree is removed only when `git status --porcelain` is empty (ignored files don't count), and its branch is deleted only when it has no commits of its own. The branch meant is the one the worktree ended on: its own `talk/cli_…`, or a branch the run made and switched to with `git-branch-create` — then `talk/cli_…` is deleted when every commit on it is also on the checkout's `HEAD` or on that branch. Anything kept is named on stderr in text mode — `Kept worktree <dir> (branch <branch>): it has uncommitted changes.` (or `git could not say whether it is clean` / `it could not be removed`), `Removed worktree <dir>; kept branch <branch>: it has commits of its own.`, plus `Also kept branch talk/cli_…: it has commits of its own.` when it applies — and `--json` and the ndjson `result` frame gain an optional `workspace` (`dir`, `branch`, `kept`, `branchKept`) on every run that used its own worktree; the protocol stays 2. Uncommitted work is never force-removed. Making the worktree fails closed and never falls back to the checkout: an unborn HEAD (`task run could not make its worktree: this repo has no commit yet (HEAD is unborn)`), a git error such as a failing post-checkout hook (git-lfs or husky without their tools; the line shown is git's `fatal:` / `error:` line, else the hook's own last line), an unusable base dir, or a start subdirectory the worktree lacks (`task run's worktree has no <dir>: the worktree is made from HEAD, so an untracked, ignored or uncommitted directory is not in it`) each exit 1 with one scrubbed `corvidinho: …` line (`--json`: `{ ok: false, error }` on stdout) and `hint: pass --here to run in this checkout`, make no model call, and remove the branch or worktree a failed `git worktree add -b` left behind. SIGINT / SIGTERM are now hooked before the worktree is made: a signal during creation (Ctrl-C at a terminal included, which also kills git mid-checkout) exits 130 with `cancelled while making the task worktree` and `hint: nothing ran and nothing was left behind; run it again`. The #324 SAFE-3.a refusal for a local run now reads `[operator] SAFE-3.a: <tools> allowlisted but not offered: a local CLI run has no role session (the CLI half of SAFE-3.a is not built yet)` — the local CLI still gets no shell, runners or Fledge runs, in its own worktree or with `--here`. No new env var or config key. Known limits: there is no sweeper, so kept worktrees pile up under `.corvid-worktrees` until removed by hand (each is named at the end of its run); in a JS project the verify lane can fail in a fresh worktree until its dependencies are installed there (use `--here`); exporting any of the four child stamps above in your shell, or setting one in a `.env` in the start directory (Bun loads it into a local run), silently makes local runs work in place (this alone cannot open the shell: such a run stays in the checkout, and the SAFE-3.a gate also needs the owner's role, a chat / ask / session / work surface stamp and the top of the talk worktree named for `CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_SESSION_ID` as its cwd); each creation runs `git worktree prune` in the repo, which drops the admin entries of other unlocked worktrees whose directories are missing (on an unmounted drive, say), and runs the repo's own post-checkout hook, as Discord talks do; "commits of its own" is measured against the checkout's current `HEAD`, so switching branches mid-run can keep a branch that could have gone.
- **A failed Discord run now says why: the owner's own run answers with one plain, scrubbed reason line, anyone else's says `That didn't work — the owner has been told.` while the owner gets the reason by DM, and every failure logs its reason** — [#340](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/340) (DISCORD-3.b captured in this PR, DISCORD-3.a, DISCORD-15.a, AGENT-9, AGENT-10, SAFE-6, SAFE-12, SAFE-13, SAFE-14.a; REQ-discord-032, REQ-agent-032, REQ-discord-079, REQ-discord-353): Leif asked `show me a gif of a dog` and got back `session sess_be8e982b6df84eb4 failed (exit 1)`, with nothing in the reply, the footer or the bridge log saying why — every Discord surface posted `session <id> failed (exit N)` / `failed (exit N)`, or a spawn's raw thrown message. Now chat and ask-pick / Answer-form resumes, `/session start`, `/work` and schedule result posts all go through one helper (new `src/discord/failure-reason.ts`). The reason is harness text only, taken in this order: the `task run` result's new `error` field — the AGENT-10 no-provider notice; for a failed model call a line from `modelCallFailedLine` (`The model call failed (401 Unauthorized from api.openai.com)`, `The model call timed out (<host>)`, `The model call failed (network error reaching <host>)`, `The model call failed (malformed reply from <host>)`, `The model call failed (<entry> needs <KEY>, which is not set)`), built from the failure kind, the status code's standard name and the provider's host, never the provider's reply body or a key; `Verification failed after <n> retries`; `Verification failed when re-run over what approving and archiving its own SpecSync change wrote` — else the AGENT-10 notice for the spawning process's tier (`CORVIDINHO_LLM_TIER`, default `tool`: the bridge's, or the daemon's for a schedule it ran), else the last meaningful line of the child's stderr (the spawn client now keeps its last 4000 characters), else `The run failed (exit N) without saying why` (`The run was interrupted before it finished` for exit 130 or a cancelled task). The run's summary (model text) and tool output are never used. The line is SAFE-6 scrubbed first (a URL's `user:pass@` is dropped too, keeping the host), then ANSI codes, stack frames, Bun source excerpts and runtime banners are dropped, the last error-like line is kept, host paths are cut to `…/<last segment>`, `@everyone` / `@here` are defanged, and it is cut to one line of at most 200 characters, at a sentence end when it can (so with no model set the owner sees `No model provider is configured: CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL is not set.`; the how-to sentence stays in `/status` and the start-up line). Every failure logs `[discord] run failed (<chat|ask|session|work>, exit N): <reason>` (no `, exit N` when the run threw), or `[scheduler] run failed (schedule <id>, exit N): <reason>`. The owner's own run (the DISCORD-15.a owner check; for a schedule, one the live owner created while not muted or deny-listed (the DISCORD-SCHEDULE-1.a owner stamp)) gets the reason as its reply body, in whatever channel it ran. Anyone else's gets `That didn't work — the owner has been told.` only once the bridge has DMed the owner `❌ A run failed (<surface> in <#channel>): <reason>` through the gateway DM the spend DMs use — one DM per reason per hour, kept in memory (a bridge restart resets it), and a DM that did not go out is not remembered, so the next failure tries again; with no owner configured, no DM path yet or a DM that did not go out, the reply is only `That didn't work.`. `/session start` and `/work` keep their heads on a thrown spawn (`Session \`<id>\` failed: <line>`, `Work \`<id>\` failed: <line>`), and a thrown run's progress status reads `❌ <line>` (the #337 Stop button still clears). A schedule's run row keeps the posted line as `summary` and `failed (exit N): <reason>` as `error`, so `corvidinho daemon`'s `run.finished` carries the reason; the daemon has no Discord, so a run it claims posts nothing and DMs nobody: the reason is in the `[scheduler]` log line, the run row's `error` and `run.finished`, and a failed run of a schedule someone else created keeps `That didn't work.` as its row's `summary`, which a bridge later shows as the context of that schedule's 5th-failure pause ask (a bridge-claimed 5th failure's pause ask, too, now carries the DISCORD-3.b line as context instead of `failed (exit N)`, REQ-discord-353); a channel-less schedule someone else created posts nothing but the owner is still DMed; a schedule run whose spawn throws still posts nothing (its reason is now logged, and the 5th failure's pause ask pings the owner as before); a run abandoned at shutdown tells nobody. `TaskResult.error` is optional (protocol stays 2). Unchanged: asks, stops and spend-cap stops (a cap stop is not a failure); a failed run that ends with its own stuck question posts that question as its why, with no extra line or DM; the `state=… verified=… attempts=…` plumbing stays in the footer (DISCORD-3.a); worktree and project refusals (`Could not isolate worktree …` in chat, `❌ worktree: …` on a pick or Answer resume, `… failed to isolate worktree: …` on `/session start` and `/work`) still show their error to whoever asked. No env var, config key, slash command or schema change. Known limits: a failed WATCH run's GitHub summary comment still posts the run's summary, which for a model failure is `LLM HTTP <status>: <provider body>`, scrubbed (a follow-up); the stderr fallback is a heuristic (it reaches only the owner, after the scrub); REQ-cli-079's text still describes a daemon no-provider schedule run's row as the plain `failed (exit 1)` line, while its `error` now reads `failed (exit 1): <reason>`.

### Real dev teammate (M3)

- **A run whose final reply is only a plan, or a short "Done."-style or empty reply, with nothing changed now gets one nudge back to the same model; a second such reply stands, with an operator note** — [#335](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/335) (AGENT-17 nudge half, AGENT-16, AUTONOMY-1, AUTONOMY-7, SAFE-13; REQ-agent-087): a model could end a run by announcing a plan ("I'll update README.md, then run the tests.") or claiming "Done." without changing anything, and that stood as the answer. Now, in every run that uses the tool loop — Discord chat, ask picks and Answer forms, `/session start`, `/work`, schedules, the CLI and the `delegate` workers of a local CLI lead (WATCH runs, `council` voices and the workers of a Discord, `/work` or schedule lead act as community, so their catalog holds no state-changing tool and they are never nudged) — the final reply that would stand (this reply, or, when it is empty, the text an earlier round gave beside its tool calls) is checked by a narrow English heuristic (`stallKind`, `src/agent/loop-guards.ts`). `done-claim`: an empty reply, or a whole reply of at most 60 characters that is a "Done."-style claim ("Done.", "All done!", "Task complete.", "I've done it.", "Changes made."). `plan`: a reply of at most 600 characters in which every sentence is a plan step, the first opening with "I'll / I will / I'm going to / Let me" plus a work verb, or a "Plan:" heading. Never a stall: a `?` (a clarifying question, AUTONOMY-1), a code fence, "let me know" and other offers, declines ("can't", "won't", "sorry" …, AUTONOMY-7), toy / demo / joke, deferrals ("later", "next time", "from now on", "going forward"), answers and social replies; and a plan is not a stall when the task text anywhere — chat context and issue bodies included — asks for a plan or for nothing to change yet ("plan", "approach", "outline", "how would you …", "what you'd …", "don't change … yet", "without editing …"). The nudge fires only when that reply stalls, the tier is not read, SAFE-13 has not tripped, that round's catalog offers a state-changing tool (`files-write` / `-edit` / `-delete`, `git-branch-create` / `-commit` / `-push`, GitHub and Discord writes, `memory-forget` / `memory-override`, `delegate`, the shell and runners, `fledge-run`, the four SpecSync change tools, or any Fledge plugin command — so a tool-tier chat with no such tool allowlisted is never nudged), and nothing changed in the run: no tool result in any attempt changed anything (here a successful `memory-store` or `memory-forget-me` counts as a change), no tool ran whose edits no result reports (shell, Fledge, a worker), and, where there is a git tree, the verify gate's real git diff since the run's baseline is empty (a diff git cannot read counts as changed). The run's first such reply gets one user message to the same model in the same conversation — `[Corvidinho harness — AGENT-17] Your reply only described a plan, and nothing has changed in this run yet.` (or `… Your reply said the work is done (or said nothing), but nothing has changed in this run: no file edits and no other changes.`) `If the task needs changes, make them now with the tools you have. If it needs none, reply with what you checked and found instead.`, plus for a plan `If you were asked only for a plan, or not to change anything yet, change nothing: reply with the plan as your answer.` and, when `ask-human` is offered, `If you can't go on without the owner's choice, call ask-human.` — through the same model chain, without using up a tool round (it is one more model call, priced and capped like any other), and the `task run` event stream gets `[operator] AGENT-17: the reply was only a plan with nothing changed; nudged once (same model)` (or `… a 'Done.'-style or empty claim …`). One nudge per run, across verify retries: a later stall stands as the answer with `[operator] AGENT-17: the reply was … with nothing changed, after the nudge; the reply stands (moving to a stronger model is not built yet)`, and no ask or error. The channel only ever sees the final reply. A stop that lands while the nudge's request is in flight ends the run cancelled (#332). The AGENT-16 repeat guard, the model chain (#325) and the spend guard are unchanged; `changedState` now shares one `isStateChangingTool` predicate with the catalog check. No setting, env var or config key: the limits are constants. AGENT-17 stays partial: moving to a stronger configured model after the nudge waits on Leif deciding what "stronger" means. Known limits: English-only and deliberately narrow — "Let's …", "I need to …", "Here's the plan" and non-English stalls are never nudged; a bare "It's done." given as a real answer to a status question in a run that changed nothing gets one nudge (one extra call; "Yes, it's done." does not).

### Safe autonomy (M4)

- **At a spend cap the run no longer just stops: with an owner configured it holds that one model call and DMs the owner a money-class Approve card, and Approve plus the one-time code lets exactly that call through at the amount shown; the next call past a cap raises a new card and code** — [#334](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/334) (SAFE-8, SAFE-8.a captured in this PR, SAFE-15, SAFE-19, SAFE-20, AUTONOMY-8, SAFE-14.a, AGENT-11; REQ-agent-198, REQ-discord-198, REQ-agent-098, REQ-agent-114): until now a priced call that would pass the total cap (`CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD`) or its provider's cap (`CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD`, #328) was never sent, and the run ended `blocked` on an operator-only `spend-cap` ask (`… Replying can't lift the cap — this needs the operator.`): raising or removing the cap and restarting, or waiting until earlier spend left the 24 h window, were the only ways past it. Now, when an owner is configured, the spend guard of every `task run` (`createSpendGuard` with the `approval` hook `createTaskExecute` now passes) holds the call and records a `spend` request, class `money`, on the #316 approvals engine in the shared DB, so any process on the data dir raises it — Discord chat, ask picks and Answer forms, `/session start`, `/work`, schedules, WATCH, `corvidinho daemon`, `delegate` / `council` workers and a local `task run` — and the running bridge's card engine (new `spend` kind, `src/discord/spend-card.ts`, audit prefix `spend-cap`) DMs it to the owner. First, as quoted data: `Asked by <Discord user id, or local> on <cli | discord:<session> | watch:<id>>, project <label>` (the project's label, never a host path), each tripped cap's 24 h spend when the call paused, `Approve lets only this one call through, at this amount; the next call past the cap asks again (SAFE-8.a).`, and the run's task (SAFE-6 scrubbed first, then at most 1500 characters, a cut marked `[… N more characters not shown]`). Then the card: title `Spend past a cap — asks first (SAFE-8) · from <surface>`, **Action** `send one model call to <model> via <provider>`, **Target** the tripped cap(s) (`total`, `provider:<id>` or both — one card per paused call, even past two caps) and **Amount** `~$<estimate> (this one call's estimate)`. Spending past a cap is a money action, so **Approve** DMs the SAFE-19 one-time code (it works once, only for this card, for 2 minutes) and only the code typed into **Enter code** counts; Approve itself only records the decision (`Approved by you — the waiting run sends exactly this one call; the next call past the cap asks again (SAFE-8.a).`). The waiting run reads the decision from the shared DB every second, uses the approval once, and calls `SpendLedger.reserveApproved`, which re-runs the cap check in one IMMEDIATE transaction and records one row at the amount the card showed; it records nothing, and the call is not sent, when the estimate is now above that amount or when the call would by then also pass a cap the card did not show (outcome `changed` — say other runs took the total past its cap while a provider cap's card was open: an approval counts only for what its card showed). The reply is still settled to the provider-reported cost, and the next call past a cap goes through the normal check again and raises a new card and code (SAFE-8.a; there is no "allow the rest of this run"). While it waits the run emits one Text event with no amounts — `[operator] AUTONOMY-8: waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card with the one-time code (one model call past a spend cap, SAFE-8.a; request <id>; no answer by <ISO time> means no, and nothing is spent — the running Discord bridge DMs the card to the owner; with no bridge running it lapses).` — which the Discord live status shows as `waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card`, and after an approval `[operator] AUTONOMY-8: the owner approved request <id>; sending that one call (SAFE-8.a: the next call past the cap asks again).`. A card stays open 4 minutes (under the 5-minute council voice cap and the 10-minute per-request timeout, which includes the wait). Deny, no answer in 4 minutes (`Expired — no answer, so nothing was spent.`), a code typed after that, the call's abort (the run being stopped, or its request timing out; a stop that lands as the owner approves wins), a waiting process that is gone (a Discord `stop` or Stop button kills the run's process tree; the card records `<pid>:<proc start>` and the bridge closes it: `Closed — nobody is waiting for this any more, so nothing was spent.`), an approval that no longer matches, or a card that could not be raised are each a no (SAFE-20): nothing is sent or spent, and the run ends `blocked` on a `spend-cap` ask that keeps the amounts and the `Stopped at cap: …` marker, says what the card came to (`The owner denied Approve card <id>, so the call was not sent and nothing was spent (SAFE-20).`, `No answer on Approve card <id> in time, …`, `The wait on Approve card <id> was cut short (the run was stopped or its request timed out), …`, `The owner approved Approve card <id>, but by then the call would also pass a cap the card did not show, …` or `Approve card <id> could not be raised or read (<why>), …`) and names both ways on — `To continue, ask again — the next call past the cap raises a new card and code — or the operator raises <the setting> …, or waits until earlier spend leaves the 24h window.` — without the "Replying can't lift the cap" note. The owner gets those details through the existing spend-stop DM (once per cap episode); every channel post, the requester and the run's summary still see only `💸 Work is paused for budget.` (SAFE-14.a). A run has at most one card open (its next paused call waits behind it); runs paused at once — parallel chats, council voices, delegate workers — each get their own card, and no run is refused because another's card is open. A card wait cut short by the per-request timeout is still a cap stop, never a model timeout, so #325's fallback chain never routes around it. The engine writes its SAFE-5 rows under the `spend-cap` prefix and the request ends `used`; no extra audit row. No card is raised, and the 0.0.37 operator-action ask comes at once, with no owner configured (nobody can approve), an invalid cap setting or an unreadable ledger; calls at an unknown price get the card since #339 (below). No schema bump, table, env var or config key. Known limits: an approved call's real cost can exceed the card's amount when its reply runs past the estimate's 4096-token reply reserve (the next call is checked again); one card and code per call past a cap can mean many cards in one run; a card wait holds its `task run` process (and a daemon schedule tick) for up to 4 minutes, so with no bridge on the data dir — a CLI-only or daemon-only box — every capped call waits out the 4 minutes and then stops; a council voice approved late in its wait has about a minute left of its 5-minute cap.
- **A call to a model with no known price now stops and asks on the same spend card, showing the amount as unknown (never $0), when a cap covers it; owner spend lines read `$X + unknown`; a WATCH run stopped at a cap now reaches the owner by DM; and a schedule's spend-cap stop gets the owner's Continue** — [#339](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/339) (SAFE-16, SAFE-16.a captured in this PR, AUTONOMY-8, SAFE-8.a, SAFE-14.a, AUTONOMY-6.a; REQ-agent-199, REQ-discord-199, REQ-watch-099, REQ-agent-098, REQ-agent-114, REQ-agent-198, REQ-discord-086, REQ-discord-198, REQ-discord-606, REQ-watch-086): in 0.0.37, and in #334 as first merged, a call to an unpriced model under a cap that covered it (the total cap, its provider's cap, or both) stopped at once with an operator ask and no card (`Spend cap can't be enforced (SAFE-8): model "<model>" has no known price, …`); a WATCH run stopped at a cap reached nobody but its `Work is paused for budget.` GitHub comment (the v0.0.36 rollup gap); and a schedule's spend-cap stop had **Cancel** only. Now, with an owner configured, such a call is held for the #334 spend card: title `Spend at an unknown price — asks first (SAFE-16.a) · from <surface>`, **Target** every cap that covers the call, **Amount** `unknown (no known price for this model; never counted as free)` — never a dollar figure — and before it, as quoted data, who asked and where, the project label, each covering cap's 24 h spend, `This model has no known price, so this call's cost is unknown: it is never counted as $0. Approve lets only this one call through; the next call at an unknown price asks again (SAFE-16.a).` and the task. Approve plus the code (outcome line `Approved by you — the waiting run sends exactly this one call; its cost stays unknown (never $0), and the next call at an unknown price asks again (SAFE-16.a).`) sends exactly that call and records it in `spend_ledger` with the new status `unknown` — estimate and cost 0, so it adds nothing to the priced spend the 80% and 100% checks compare, and it is never shown as $0 — settled with the reply's token counts, or as `failed` on an HTTP error (a network error leaves it `unknown`); the next such call raises a new card and code. Any no — deny, a lapse, a late code, a stop, the request timeout — sends and records nothing and ends the run `blocked` on the unpriced `spend-cap` ask: `Spend at an unknown price (SAFE-16.a): model "<model>" has no known price, so I held its call under <the cap> and asked the owner on a spend Approve card showing the amount as unknown (never counted as $0). <what the card came to> Stopped at cap: <scope>. To continue, ask again — the next call at an unknown price raises a new card and code — or the operator switches <the model setting> to a priced model or unsets CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD (or removes the <id> entry of CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD) …`, with no "Replying can't lift the cap" note. Unchanged: with no cap covering the call it just runs, unrecorded; with no owner configured (or through `withSpendCap`) the operator ask comes at once, before the ledger opens; there is no price override (the price table stays frozen and no env var or config key sets a price). Priced and unknown-price cards share one wait, at most one open per run. SAFE-16 on owner lines: the ledger window now counts `unknownCalls`, carried by cap trips, warnings, the bridge's warning outbox and `task run` result frames (whole positive counts only, at most 1e9), and the new `formatSpend` shows `$X + unknown` while the 24 h window holds such a call — in `corvidinho doctor` (whose `spend` and `spend provider:<id>` lines also add `, N at an unknown price`), the owner's `/status` lines, the 80% warning and its DM, the cap-stop ask and its DM, and the card's context; the doctor and `/status` unpriced-model notes now add `(each call on a spend card showing the amount as unknown, SAFE-16.a)` / `(card shows the amount as unknown)`. Everyone else still sees only `Work is paused for budget.`. AUTONOMY-8 on every surface: a WATCH run that stops at a cap (a card that came to no, a bad setting or ledger) is now, with an owner configured, recorded in `watch_owner_asks` with reason `spend-cap` and logged without amounts (with no owner nothing is recorded; only the no-owner log line below is written) — `[watch] spend-cap stop <repo>#<n> id=…: queued for the owner's Discord DM (AUTONOMY-8)`, or `… the owner's Discord DM could not be sent — no Discord bridge is running on this data dir (CORVIDINHO_DATA_DIR); it is sent if one starts within a day; …` and the no-owner variant, these two each ending `GitHub shows only that work is paused for budget (SAFE-14.a) (AUTONOMY-8)` or, when no summary comment was posted (an assignment or review request, or a mention whose auto-ack failed), `no comment on GitHub carries it (SAFE-14.a) (AUTONOMY-8)` — and the bridge DMs the owner `💸 Work is paused for budget. Only you see these details (SAFE-14.a).`, then `GitHub <repo>#<n>: <url>` and the quoted, scrubbed details, once per cap episode through the same `claimCapPing` as chat and schedule stops (`[discord] WATCH spend-cap stop …: owner DMed (AUTONOMY-8)`; a stop whose episode was already told is dropped with `… the owner was already told about this cap episode; not DMed again (AUTONOMY-8)`). A failed DM, or a shutdown that hands back a DM still in flight, gives back both the ask and its episode claim, so a restart still tells the owner; a stop not delivered within a day is given up with a log line, as for stuck asks. The GitHub comment is unchanged. Schedules (AUTONOMY-6.a): a spend-cap stop's post now carries **Continue** next to **Cancel**. Only the live owner may press Continue (the creator's press, and a Choose / Answer submit on it, gets `This choice isn’t for you (or it was already answered).`); it closes the question with the new `ask_outcome` `continued`, hands no answer to the next run (so the stop's amounts never reach a prompt) and answers privately `Continuing — the schedule's next run goes ahead, and any call past a spend cap (or at an unknown price) asks you first on a spend card with a one-time code (SAFE-8.a, SAFE-16.a).`. Continue starts no run: the next due run goes ahead, and each of its calls past a cap still needs its own card. The creator can still Cancel, and the post still says only `💸 Work is paused for budget.`. Daemon and CLI runs already record their cards for the bridge to DM (#334), now for unknown-price calls too; a CLI run's stop after a no is printed in the terminal. No schema bump or new table (`spend_ledger.status` and `schedule_runs.ask_outcome` are TEXT); no env var or config key. Known limits: a run on an unpriced model under a cap asks once per call, one card and code each; approved unknown-price calls are not added to the caps, so their real cost can exceed a cap without the priced total showing it (the owner's lines say `+ unknown`); after a no the unpriced ask names one cap (the total if set, else the provider's); Continue does not start a run, so the schedule still waits for its next due slot.

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.38** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update: they read the environment and allowlist at start and spawn `task run` from the updated checkout. The bridge registers the new `spend` card kind — a bridge still on 0.0.37 never DMs the spend cards the updated `task run` raises, so every capped call waits out its 4 minutes and stops with `No answer on Approve card <id> in time …` — and brings the Stop button (#337), the failed-run reason, owner DM and log lines (#340), the WATCH spend-cap DM delivery and the schedule **Continue** (#339), and the `--here` spawn argv (#338); `corvidinho daemon` brings `--here`, the `[scheduler] run failed …` lines and the reason in `run.finished`'s `error` (`failed (exit N): <reason>`); `github watch` brings `--here` and the spend-cap hand-over to `watch_owner_asks` (#339). A `task run` that a not-yet-restarted spawner starts without `--here` still works in place (it is a role-session, WATCH, Discord or delegate child), so the update itself never nests a worktree. No slash command is added or changed, and the NDJSON protocol stays 2 (the result's new `error` and `workspace` are optional). **Schema stays v15** (`SCHEMA_VERSION` 15, `SCRUB_RULES_VERSION` 3): this release has no forward-only migration step, records nothing new in `schema_meta`, and adds no table, column or index, not even by module DDL (0.0.37's #328 idempotent `ALTER TABLE` that added `spend_alerts.scope`, and its `idx_spend_ledger_provider_ts`, are unchanged). It only writes new values into existing TEXT columns: `spend_ledger.status` `unknown` (#339; estimate and cost 0), `approval_requests` rows of kind `spend`, class `money` (#334, in the v14 approvals tables, their task text a SAFE-6 scrub target like every card's), `schedule_runs.ask_outcome` `continued` (#339) and `watch_owner_asks.reason` `spend-cap` (#339). Forget is unchanged from 0.0.36: it takes Approve on the owner's DM card plus the one-time code. v0.0.38 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main; the bridge's restart post links `…/releases/tag/v0.0.38`, which 404s until release.yml has created that Release.
- New and changed operator surface: no new env var or config key. New flag `--here` on `corvidinho task run` (#338; read only before the first `--`; not a global flag). `WORKTREE_BASE_DIR` (existing; unset = `.corvid-worktrees` next to the project; for a local `task run`, next to the repo's top level, even when started in a subdirectory) now also places local `task run` worktrees. A configured owner (`CORVIDINHO_OWNER_DISCORD_ID`, or the allowlist file's `[owner] discord_id`; a missing or non-snowflake id means no owner) now decides two things: with one, a call past a cap (or a call at an unknown price under a cap that covers it) first asks on the owner's spend card (#334, #339; an invalid cap setting or an unreadable ledger still stops with the operator ask at once, with no card) and other people's failed runs DM the owner their reason through the bridge (#340; `corvidinho daemon` wires no owner DM and posts nothing, so a failed run it claims tells nobody: its reason is only in the `[scheduler]` log, the run row and `run.finished`); with none, a cap stop is the 0.0.37 operator ask at once and others' failed runs answer only `That didn't work.`. `CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD` and `CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD` keep their form and bounds; a cap that covers an unpriced model now means one card per call to it, and there is no price override. The internal stamps `CORVIDINHO_ACTING_IS_ADMIN`, `CORVIDINHO_WATCH_SESSION_ID`, `CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_SESSION_ID` and `CORVIDINHO_DELEGATE_DEPTH` now also keep a `task run` in place — never export them in your shell or a project `.env`. Fixed constants with no knob: a spend card stays open 4 minutes, the waiting run reads its decision every 1 s, at most one card is open per run, the card quotes at most 1500 characters of the task, the one-time code still lasts 2 minutes; a failure reason is one line of at most 200 characters, the owner is DMed once per reason per hour (in memory), and a failed run's stderr tail is 4000 characters; AGENT-17's limits are 60 characters ("Done."-style) and 600 (plan), one nudge per run. New log and operator lines: `[discord] run failed (<surface>, exit N): <reason>` and `[scheduler] run failed (schedule <id>, exit N): <reason>` (#340); `[discord] run <run id> of session <session id> stopped by <user id>` now also for Stop button presses (#337); `[watch] spend-cap stop … (AUTONOMY-8)` and `[discord] WATCH spend-cap stop …` lines, and `[discord] WATCH ask delivery (stuck asks, spend-cap stops) failed: …` in place of the stuck-ask-only failure line (#339); in `task run` output `[operator] AUTONOMY-8: waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card with the one-time code …` and `[operator] AUTONOMY-8: the owner approved request <id>; …` (#334, #339), `[operator] AGENT-17: …` (#335), `Working in a new worktree …`, `Kept worktree …` / `Removed worktree …; kept branch …`, the refusals `corvidinho: task run could not make its worktree: …` / `corvidinho: task run's worktree has no …` with `hint: pass --here to run in this checkout` (exit 1) and `corvidinho: cancelled while making the task worktree` with `hint: nothing ran and nothing was left behind; run it again` (exit 130) (#338) and the reworded `[operator] SAFE-3.a: … a local CLI run has no role session (the CLI half of SAFE-3.a is not built yet)` (#338); the owner DM `❌ A run failed (<surface> in <#channel>): <reason>` (#340).
- Needs operator action: (1) Restart the bridge with this update (above): until it runs 0.0.38, spend cards are never DMed and every capped call lapses after 4 minutes. Spend cards also need the bridge on the same `CORVIDINHO_DATA_DIR` as the run and an owner who accepts DMs from server members; on a CLI-only or daemon-only box with a cap and an owner configured, each capped call now waits out the 4 minutes before it stops. (2) Local `task run` (#338): in a git repo it now works in a new worktree made from `HEAD`, so a script, job or habit that expects the run to edit the current checkout (or to see uncommitted files, a local `.env` file (the run's own environment still comes from the directory it started in) or installed `node_modules`) must pass `--here`; in a JS project the new worktree has no `node_modules` and nothing installs them, so its verify lane may fail unless the lane installs them itself: pass `--here` there; a repo with no commit yet, or whose post-checkout hook fails (git-lfs or husky with nothing installed), a start directory that is untracked, ignored or uncommitted, or a worktree base dir that cannot be made (`WORKTREE_BASE_DIR`, else `.corvid-worktrees` in the repo's parent directory, which must then be writable) now refuses with exit 1 and `hint: pass --here to run in this checkout`; each new worktree also runs `git worktree prune` in the repo, which drops the records of other unlocked worktrees whose directories are gone; kept worktrees and `talk/cli_…` branches are never swept — each is named at the end of its run; remove them by hand when done. (3) Unpriced models (#339): a model with no known price under a cap that covers it now asks the owner on a card for every call; to stop the cards, switch the tier's setting to a priced model or drop the cap that covers it (no price override exists). (4) Schedules stopped at a cap (#339): the owner can press **Continue** on the stop's post to let the next due run go ahead (each call past a cap still asks on a card); nothing else changes for existing schedules.
- Other behaviour operators will notice: a run at a cap waits (up to 4 minutes) with the status `waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card` instead of stopping at once, the owner gets a `spend` card DM per paused call, and the channel still sees only `💸 Work is paused for budget.` after a no (#334); owner spend lines read `$X + unknown` once a call at an unknown price was approved in the last 24 h, doctor adds `, N at an unknown price`, schedule spend-cap posts carry **Continue** and **Cancel**, and WATCH cap stops now DM the owner the details with the GitHub link (#339); every Discord progress message shows a red **Stop** button while its run goes (#337); failed runs no longer post `session <id> failed (exit N)`: the owner's own failed run posts its scrubbed reason line in that channel (public channels included), anyone else's posts `That didn't work — the owner has been told.` and the owner gets `❌ A run failed …` DMs, and every failure logs its reason (#340); some runs make one extra model call when the model only planned or said "Done." with nothing changed, shown by an `[operator] AGENT-17` line in `task run` output (#335); a local `task run` prints where it works and what it kept, and the SAFE-3.a refusal line for local runs is reworded (#338).
- Rollback to 0.0.37: the schema is v15 on both, so 0.0.37 opens the DB as it is and its backup and `backup restore` version check does not trip on a 0.0.38 snapshot. It reads `unknown` spend rows as calls at $0 (counted in the call totals, never `+ unknown`; the priced spend and the caps are unaffected); its card engine has no `spend` kind, so it neither DMs nor closes `spend` requests (only a 0.0.38 run ever waits on one); it treats an `ask_outcome` of `continued` like `cancelled` (no answer handed on); and a 0.0.37 bridge DMs a pending WATCH `spend-cap` row in its stuck-ask format — the thread link and `💸 Work is paused for budget.` without the details — with no once-per-cap-episode check. Behaviour returns to 0.0.37's: a cap stop is the operator-only ask again (no card; unpriced models stop at once), a WATCH cap stop reaches nobody but its GitHub comment, a schedule's cap stop has **Cancel** only, there is no Stop button (the stop words remain; a Stop button already on a message stays there — on a `/session start` or `/work` progress message, and on one whose run the downgrade restart interrupted, since 0.0.37's interrupted notice edits only the embed — and a press on it gets no answer, so Discord shows its own interaction-failed notice), failed runs post `session <id> failed (exit N)` / `failed (exit N)` again, there is no AGENT-17 nudge, and `task run` works in the current checkout again (0.0.37 ignores `--here`, so a not-yet-restarted 0.0.38 spawner still works against it). Worktrees and `talk/cli_…` branches that 0.0.38 kept stay until removed by hand.

## 0.0.37

### Talk anywhere (M2)

- **A Discord session now runs one thing at a time: a message sent while its run is going waits for it, `stop` or `cancel` from the person who asked (or from the owner, replying to the run's progress message) kills the run, and the messages that were waiting still run after it, in order** — [#332](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/332) (AGENT-3.a in part, AGENT-3.b captured in this PR, AGENT-3, SAFE-20; REQ-discord-301, REQ-discord-302, REQ-discord-002, REQ-discord-044): a message sent to a session while that session's run was going started a second run of the same session in parallel, and nothing on Discord could stop a run (`cancel` only cleared open questions). Every Discord run path now takes a turn on its session's first-in-first-out queue (`SessionRunControl`, new `src/discord/run-control.ts`) before anything of it runs: chat messages that start or continue a session, ask **Choose** picks and **Answer** form submits that resume one, and `/session start` and `/work` (which hold their new session's turn, so the requester's @mention in that channel while the run goes waits too). Runs of different sessions still go in parallel. A waiting message gets no new indicator — its usual progress message appears when its turn starts — and its REQ-discord-311 in-flight row is recorded as soon as it starts waiting; after the wait it goes on exactly as if it had just arrived, so it sees the earlier answer and the thin-ack, pending-ask, cancel and injection rules apply to it. Because `/admin` list changes and mutes are live, the gates are checked again after the wait: a message whose session ended or idled out, whose sender was forgotten, muted or deny-listed, or whose channel or thread was dropped from the allowlist or deny-listed meanwhile runs nothing, posts nothing (no refusal notice) and clears its row, and the rate limit is not counted twice; a waiting pick or Answer submit is re-checked for its press and session channels and its presser. A message whose whole text (mentions left out, any case, trailing `.` / `!` allowed) is `stop` or `cancel` stops the run in flight when it comes from the requester in their own session, or as a reply to that run's progress message (the chat progress message, the Choose stub a pick resumed in, or the `/session start` / `/work` progress message) in that message's own channel from the requester or the configured owner — a new `stop_run` route checked right after the channel gate and before the thread and bot-message lookups, still behind the actor gate and the mute / rate gate, so a muted or deny-listed requester cannot stop by text while the owner still can by reply. `stop it`, `please stop` and anyone else's reply are ordinary messages. A stop never waits in the queue: it aborts the run once (a second `stop` gets the same ack and aborts nothing more), which kills the run's whole process tree through the spawn client, answers each stop message with `⏹ Stopping the run.`, turns the progress message into `⏹ Stopped` in the error colour with the DISCORD-15 / 15.a footer (model and time for everyone, tokens and cost only on the owner's own runs), drops any question the stopped run raised, and logs `[discord] run <run id> of session <session id> stopped by <user id>`. An Approve/Deny card pass runs once the stopped run has finished, so a #316 / #319 card the killed run was waiting on closes as a no at once (SAFE-20: nobody waits for it any more). A stopped `/session start` answers its head lines and `⏹ Stopped`; a stopped `/work` is recorded `failed` with summary `stopped`, opens no PR and says `PR: not opened — the run was stopped.`; a stop that lands after the agent exited but before the PR step still opens no PR and gives that line, and a run that had finished cleanly is then recorded `failed` / `stopped` too. Messages that were waiting behind a stopped run still run afterwards, in order (AGENT-3.b). With nothing of the session running, `cancel` still clears the session's open questions and `stop` is an ordinary message; while a run is going, a `cancel` message stops it and leaves open questions and their buttons open (a `cancel` typed in an ask's **Answer** form still clears them and stops nothing). Stopping the bridge aborts every run in flight and posts nothing; waiting messages do not start and keep their in-flight rows, so the next start marks those replies interrupted, and a `/work` cut that way stays `running` until restart recovery fails it. The queue lives in memory: no env var, config key, slash command, table or schema change. Not built: the **Stop** button (the rest of AGENT-3.a) and stopping a schedule's run from Discord (Leif, round 13). A stop that lands while the answer is already being posted, or while `/work`'s commit / push / PR step is already running, gets the ack but that answer or PR still goes out; a pick's acting role is resolved when it is pressed, not after it waits (the tool layer re-checks the role on every call).

### Real dev teammate (M3)

- **A tier's model list is now a fallback chain: when a model fails or is retired the run moves on to the next configured model at once and says so — in an operator line, a closing note, the Discord footer and the service logs — while a spend-cap stop, the run's own stop or a denied card never fails over** — [#325](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/325) (landed as 7090656; GitHub shows the PR closed, not merged, after a merge API error; AGENT-11, AGENT-13, AGENT-10, SAFE-8, SAFE-16, DISCORD-15.a; REQ-agent-080, REQ-cli-080, REQ-discord-080, REQ-watch-080, REQ-plugins-080, REQ-agent-179, REQ-agent-007, REQ-agent-079, REQ-discord-457): since #320, `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` and `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` took comma lists of `kind:model` entries but only the first entry was ever called, so a failing, rate-limited or retired model failed the run. Each `task run` process now builds one chain for its tier and sends every model call through it — every tool-loop round, the read tier's single chat and every verify retry. A call that fails as a model — an HTTP error of any status (404 / 410 for a retired or missing model, but also 400, 401 and 429), a network error, the per-request timeout (10 minutes) or a malformed reply (not JSON, or no assistant message) — goes, as the same request, to the next entry at once, with no retry and no backoff, and the process keeps that entry for every later round and retry. Nothing is stored: the next process (the next Discord message, schedule run or WATCH event) tries the head once again. A later entry whose kind needs a key that is not set is skipped without a call (reason `<KEY> is not set`); a head entry with no usable key still ends the run with the AGENT-10 no-provider notice, as in 0.0.36, and does not fail over. A SAFE-8 / SAFE-14 spend-cap stop, the run's own abort (a failure that lands while it aborts included) and a Deny or lapsed must-ask card are not model failures and never fail over, so a cap is never routed around; a failure on the last entry ends the attempt as before, and the image-refusal retry still runs on a model before it fails over. Each failover emits `[operator] <a> failed (<reason>); falling back to <b>` (on stderr in text mode, a `Text` frame in NDJSON; `<reason>` is `HTTP <status>`, `timed out`, `network error`, `malformed reply` or `<KEY> is not set`, never provider output) and adds one closing note to the run's summary — `(model fallback: <a> failed (<reason>), fell back to <b>[; …])`, after a SAFE-13 note and before the role note, which stays last — that clips and Discord splits keep whole, so the chat answer, `/session start`, `/work`, a schedule's result post, a WATCH comment and `task run` output all carry it, for everyone (entry labels and reasons only, a skipped entry naming its key's env var, never a value or an amount). `task run` results gain optional `model` (the entry that answered), `usageByModel` and `modelFallback` (`from`, `to`, `reason`, optional `via`), and NDJSON `usage` frames gain `model` and `byModel`; the protocol stays 2. A `delegate` or `council` worker's failovers reach the lead's run as its own, marked `delegate worker: …` / `council worker: …`, each once. The Discord footer now names the model that answered by its entry label — `gpt-4.1 (fell back from gpt-5)` after a failover of the run's own chain (a worker's failover shows only in the note), and `anthropic:…` / `ollama:…` for non-`openai` kinds even without one — and on the owner's own runs prices each model's tokens at its own price, showing `cost unknown` (never a partial sum or $0) when any model that used tokens has no known price. A run that failed over logs one warn line, the owner's runs included: `[discord] llm.fallback: <a> failed (<reason>), fell back to <b> (session <id>)` from the bridge, `[watch] llm.fallback: … (session <id>)` from `github watch`, and a structured `llm.fallback` warn event (`sessionId`, `fallbacks`, `message`) from `corvidinho daemon`; there is no owner DM. No env var, config key, flag, slash command, protocol or schema change. Known limits: a failed schedule run's post stays `failed (exit N)` without the note; when the verify loop appends its failure text after the note (a failed or stuck run) a long reply's clip can drop it (the footer and the log line still tell); the live thinking status does not show a failover; a request a provider rejects as invalid (400) walks the whole chain, one failed call per entry; the conversation moves to the next model mid-run, so a provider that rejects another provider's history fails in turn.
- **In a repo that works through SpecSync changes, the run now opens and works a change for its edits and is not verified while a changed meaningful path has none, `/work` will not ship such a branch, the file tools refuse SpecSync's own records, and on Corvidinho itself the run approves and archives its own change once verify is green** — [#329](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/329) (AGENT-18 SpecSync clause, AGENT-18.a captured in this PR, SPECSYNC-4, AGENT-15, AGENT-16, IDENTITY-10, SAFE-1, SAFE-2, SAFE-5; REQ-agent-518, REQ-agent-519, REQ-plugins-518, REQ-plugins-519, REQ-discord-518, REQ-agent-002, REQ-agent-065, REQ-agent-086, REQ-plugins-065, REQ-plugins-083, REQ-plugins-114): nothing tied the agent's edits to a SpecSync change — a run in a repo whose `.specsync/sdd.json` requires a change for meaningful files (Corvidinho's own does) could edit and end verified with none, `/work` shipped such a branch, and the file tools could write a change folder's state, approval and review records. At planning each run now detects the repo's ways — the SpecSync change workflow (`.specsync/sdd.json` with `enabled: true`), hi criteria (a `hi/*.md` with `hi:` front matter) and Trust (`.trust.toml`) — as the union of the session base (the merge-base with the default branch, else HEAD), HEAD and the working tree, so deleting or committing a file away mid-run cannot switch one off. It emits one line such as `Repo ways (AGENT-18): SpecSync changes (.specsync/sdd.json), hi criteria (hi/).` (Trust reads `Trust (.trust.toml; its steps are not followed yet)`), and the tool loop gets one fixed prompt block: open a change before changing a file, answer its interview, fill its artifacts, never approve, review or finalize it yourself, and in a hi repo never invent criteria. New tools: `specsync-change-status` (read-only, minTier 0: tool tier and up, since the read tier offers no tools), and `specsync-change-new` / `specsync-change-answer` (mutating but not dangerous, code tier, owner runs (the owner's own schedules too, since #330) and team `/work` runs), which refuse `--root`, refuse `refused: this project's SpecSync change workflow is off (no .specsync/sdd.json with enabled: true), so there is no change to open or work; edit as usual (AGENT-18)` where the workflow is off, and record as this run's own only the change ids their own `specsync change new` spawn added, never ids from model text; in a hi repo an `acceptance_criteria` answer must cite at least one hi id that `hi export` shows captured and no id that is not (`refused: in a hi repo an acceptance_criteria answer cites the captured hi ids it meets (e.g. AGENT-18); this one cites none. Never invent criteria (AGENT-18)`, `refused: <ids> is not captured in hi/ (hi export); cite only captured criteria and never invent them (AGENT-18)`, or refused when `hi export` cannot be read). Coverage gate: where `sdd.json` sets `require_change_for_meaningful_files` (merged fail-closed across the base, HEAD and the working tree: required in any tree counts, `meaningful_paths` the union, `ignored_paths` the intersection, the more specific entry winning, SpecSync's defaults for a list left out, and an unparseable file making every path meaningful), every meaningful path in the run's real diff must be covered by an open change's `affected_paths` (a file or directory prefix; glob-shaped entries are not expanded) or by a change archived in the same diff. Otherwise the attempt fails before the verify lane runs, with `SpecSync gate: <n> changed path(s) this repo's SpecSync workflow needs a change for are not covered by an open SpecSync change (<up to 5 paths, then …>), so the run is not verified. Open one with specsync-change-new "<summary>" --kind <kind> --spec <module> --path <each path>, then answer its interview with specsync-change-answer (AGENT-18).` as the retry's feedback, and after `max_retries` the run ends `failed` with the stuck question that pings the owner; a diff that cannot be read fails closed (`SpecSync gate: could not read what changed, so SpecSync change coverage can't be checked and the run is not verified (AGENT-18).`). A repo that turns the workflow on without requiring changes gets the prompt block but no gate. `/work` runs the same check against the branch's merge-base after the tests-deleted check and before anything is committed or pushed, with the new skip reason `sdd-uncovered`: `PR: not opened — <n> changed path(s) this repo's SpecSync workflow needs a change for are not covered by a SpecSync change (<paths>); open one with specsync change new … --path <each path> (AGENT-18).
