The changes stay on branch <branch>.` (or `… could not read what changed since the branch left <base> to check SpecSync change coverage. …`). `files-write`, `files-edit` and `files-delete` now refuse, in every repo and for every role, the `*.json` files directly in `.specsync/changes/<id>/` (state, approvals, review, verification): `refused (SAFE-2): '<path>' is a SpecSync lifecycle record (state, approvals, review, verification); only the specsync change commands write it. …`; the change's `.md` artifacts and deltas stay writable (SPECSYNC-4), and `git-commit` still stages their deletion when a change is archived. AGENT-18.a: right after a green lane that shows tests ran, `runTask` settles the changes this run opened that are still open. On Corvidinho — the run's directory shares the git common dir of the checkout Corvidinho runs from (that checkout or one of its worktrees) and its `origin` is github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho, both read from disk, never from a flag — it calls `specsync-change-approve` (`specsync change approve <id> --actor corvid-agent`) and then `specsync-change-finalize` (`change check`, `change review --reviewer corvid-agent`, `change finalize`) through `runPlugin`, so the role gate, the SAFE-1 allowlist, the must-ask gate and the SAFE-5 audit all apply, then runs the lane once more over what they wrote (`Running fledge lanes run verify --non-interactive again over the SpecSync records it wrote…`); a failing second lane fails the run (`Verification failed when re-run over what approving and archiving its own SpecSync change wrote: …`). It reports `SpecSync: verify is green, so it approved and archived its own change <id> (AGENT-18.a).`, or `SpecSync: did not approve its own change <id>: <error> It stays open for a human (AGENT-18.a).` / `SpecSync: approved its own change <id> but did not archive it: <error> A human reviews and finalizes it (AGENT-18.a).`; in any other repo, a separate clone of Corvidinho included, it says `SpecSync: <ids> stays open for a human to approve, review and finalize; the agent approves and archives its own change only on Corvidinho (AGENT-18.a).`. The approve and finalize tools are dangerous (SAFE-1 allowlist, SAFE-5 audit), code tier, and never in the model's catalog (new `PluginCommand.agentTool: false`; `corvidinho plugins run` still reaches them), and their own gate refuses a `delegate` / `council` worker, WATCH runs and schedules, community runs, any repo but Corvidinho (`refused: in this repo a human approves, reviews and finalizes SpecSync changes (AGENT-18.a); only on Corvidinho itself does the agent approve and archive its own change, once verify is green`), a change this run did not open, and any moment but right after this run's green lane. The four mutating change tools count as state changes for the AGENT-16 repeat-failure guard, and the tool-surface budget moves from 8000 to 9000 tokens (REQ-plugins-114). AGENT-18 stays partial: its hi-drafting guard and its Trust steps are later work. Known limits: the owner's allowlisted shell and runners (#324) can still run `specsync change approve`, `review` or `finalize` directly; in the owner's chat, allowlisted `git-commit` / `git-push` / `github-pr-create` can ship edits during the tool loop before the gate runs (`/work` checks before it commits); a change opened by an earlier run of the same talk, or by a worker, is left for a human; on Corvidinho self-approval adds several minutes per run (`change check` runs the repo's verification commands, finalize walks the archive history, then a second lane); if the process dies between approve and finalize, the change stays approved but open for a human.

### Safe autonomy (M4)

- **The owner's own chat, `/session start` and `/work` runs may now use the allowlisted shell, language runners and Fledge lane / task runs, only at code tier and only inside that talk's own worktree — non-owners, WATCH, schedules, workers and the local CLI still never get them — and Fledge lane and task runs now start without GitHub or git credentials** — [#324](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/324) (SAFE-3.a Discord half, SAFE-21.a, SAFE-3, SAFE-21, SAFE-1, SAFE-5, AUTONOMY-9; REQ-agent-503, REQ-discord-735, REQ-watch-735, REQ-agent-501, REQ-agent-502, REQ-plugins-461): until now `shell-exec`, `node-exec`, `python-exec`, `cargo-exec`, `fledge-lanes-run` and `fledge-run` stayed out of the model's catalog even when allowlisted (SAFE-3.a pending) and ran only through operator `corvidinho plugins run`; and `fledge-lanes-run` / `fledge-run` started with only the verify lane's scrub, so `GITHUB_TOKEN` / `GH_TOKEN` were dropped but `GIT_ASKPASS`, `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`, an inherited `GH_CONFIG_DIR`, gh's `hosts.yml` and the owner's `~/.gitconfig` credential helper still reached fledge. A new gate (`src/agent/shell-gate.ts`), re-run at every attempt of a run, now offers the six (the set is renamed `SAFE3A_TOOLS`) only when all of these hold: the allowlist names the tool and the run is at code tier (`CORVIDINHO_LLM_TIER=code` in the bridge's environment, since the bridge passes no `--tier`; never at the default `tool` tier); the run is not a `delegate` / `council` worker, has a role session (so not the local CLI), is not a WATCH or scheduled run, and its surface stamp is `chat`, `ask`, `session` or `work`; the acting role, re-resolved then the way `runPlugin` resolves it, is the owner (not muted or deny-listed; team never qualifies); and the run's directory, symlinks resolved, is the top of the linked talk worktree made for this session (a `talk-<id>` directory whose git admin dir sits under `worktrees/talk-*` and whose `gitdir` points back) — the main checkout, another talk's worktree, a subdirectory, a non-git project's scoped folder and look-alikes are refused. The surface stamp is a new internal `CORVIDINHO_ACTING_SURFACE` that the spawning client always overwrites (bridge chat `chat`, an ask-button pick or Answer form continuing a talk `ask`, `/session start` `session`, `/work` `work`, schedules `schedule`, WATCH `watch`, empty when the caller names none) and that delegate workers and the verify lane drop. When the allowlist names one of the six and the gate refuses, the run's event stream carries one line per run, never part of the reply (the bridge, the daemon and `github watch` neither show nor log `Text` frames, so a Discord run's line is not in their logs): `[operator] SAFE-3.a: <tools> allowlisted but not offered: <why>` (`a delegate or council worker never gets them`, `a local CLI run has no talk worktree of its own yet`, `WATCH runs never get them`, `scheduled runs never get them`, `only the owner's chat, /session start, /work and their ask answers get them (this run: <surface>)`, `only the owner's own runs get them` or `the run is not in this talk's own worktree`). A granted call still goes through `runPlugin` — the role re-check, SAFE-1, the #319 must-ask gate (a prod command such as `kubectl get pods` in the owner's chat raises the destructive `mustask` card with the one-time code; denied, nothing runs) and the SAFE-5 audit — and through the tools' own SAFE-3 clamp, SAFE-21 refusals and credential-free env. The Fledge core builtins (`fledge-lanes-list`, `fledge-lanes-validate`, `fledge-lanes-run`, `fledge-run`) now start with the same SAFE-21.a env as `shell-exec` and the runners, for model and operator calls alike: the GitHub and git credential env vars (`GH_TOKEN`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `GIT_ASKPASS`, `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`, `GH_CONFIG_DIR`, `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` and the like) are dropped, and fledge gets `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null`, `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1`, an empty `credential.helper` and `http.extraHeader`, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, a key-less `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` and `GH_CONFIG_DIR` pointed at an empty temp dir (gh logged out); discovered `fledge-<command>` plugins and the verify gate's own lane run are unchanged. SAFE-3.a is only partly met: a local `corvidinho task run` is refused until the CLI gets a per-run worktree of its own, and an owner run in a non-git project folder gets no shell because it has no worktree. Known limits: the runners' own code and a Fledge lane or task run as the bot's Unix user and can change directory, read or write host files — Corvidinho's data dir included — in ways no lexical check sees, so keep the allowlist file and other secrets outside every talk worktree; within one attempt the surface and directory are fixed (the role is re-checked at every call).
- **Spend caps are now kept per provider as well as in total: each provider's rolling 24-hour cap and the total cap warn the owner at 80% and stop the call to ask at 100%, on their own; doctor and the owner's `/status` show every cap** — [#328](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/328) (SAFE-14, SAFE-15 in part, SAFE-8, SAFE-14.a, SAFE-16, AGENT-11; REQ-agent-114, REQ-agent-098, REQ-cli-098, REQ-cli-262, REQ-discord-098): only one cap existed, the total `CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD`. New optional `CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD` takes a comma list of `provider=USD` entries (for example `api.openai.com=3,api.anthropic.com=2`), each key the configured provider id — the endpoint host a model entry calls, with its port when not the scheme's default (`api.openai.com`, `api.anthropic.com`, `127.0.0.1:11434` for the default Ollama host), matched case-insensitively — and each amount a plain USD figure (`5`, `2.50`, `.5`, at most 1e9). Both settings are optional and independent: the total cap still applies to every call. The whole provider setting is invalid when any entry lacks `=`, is blank (a trailing comma included), has a key that is not a host, repeats a key or has a bad amount, or when a key names no configured provider (configured means used by any entry of `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` or `_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE`, fallback entries included, usable or not); an invalid setting stops every call and asks before the ledger opens, never echoing the value: `Spend cap can't be enforced (SAFE-8): CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD is set but is not a comma list of provider=USD entries for configured providers (e.g. api.openai.com=5,api.anthropic.com=2), so I stopped before calling the provider. …`. While any cap is set every priced call is recorded in the ledger, and each call's reservation is checked against the total cap and its own provider's cap in one IMMEDIATE transaction (new index `idx_spend_ledger_provider_ts` on `spend_ledger(provider, ts)`). A model with no known price stops and asks when a cap covers its call (the total cap, or its provider's cap); with no total cap and no cap for its provider it is sent and not recorded (its cost stays unknown, SAFE-16, round 13). The stop is still a `SpendCapRefusal` thrown before any request, so #325's fallback chain never routes around it. The question now names each tripped cap and carries a fixed marker right after the stop, before the operator step: a provider cap reads `Daily spend cap reached (SAFE-15): $<spent> spent on <id> in the last 24h (<n>% of its $<cap> cap), and the next call to it (~$<estimate>) would pass it, so I stopped before sending it. Stopped at cap: provider:<id>. To continue, the operator raises the <id> entry of CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD (or removes it) …`, a call past both reads `… the next provider call (~$<estimate>) would pass 2 caps, so I stopped before sending it: total — …; provider:<id> — …. Stopped at caps: total, provider:<id>. …`, and the total cap's question is unchanged apart from gaining `Stopped at cap: total.` (the unpriced-model question gains the marker too). Scopes appear only where the owner or operator looks — the owner's DM, doctor, `task run` output and daemon logs; the run summary and every channel post and WATCH comment still say only `Work is paused for budget.` (SAFE-14.a). Warnings and pings are kept per cap: `spend_alerts` gains a `scope` column (`total` or `provider:<id>`, scrubbed), each cap warns once per crossing of 80% (re-armed when its spend is seen back under 70%), several warnings go in one owner DM with one line per cap — a provider's reads `⚠️ Spend warning (SAFE-15, provider:<id>): $<spent> of the $<cap> daily cap for <id> used in the last 24h (<n>%). At that cap I stop and ask before spending more on it.` — and each cap's stop pings the owner once per episode of that cap (the scopes come from the ask, or from the `Stopped at cap` marker of a stored schedule or daemon question, and a schedule's ping key follows its provider caps). Doctor prints `spend` and then one `spend provider:<id>` line per provider cap (`$<spent> of $<cap> daily cap for <id> used in the last 24h (<n>%; <calls> provider call(s)…; CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD, SAFE-14)`, marked warn with `cap reached — calls to <id> stop and ask before they are sent` or `past the 80% warning — …`); with provider caps only, `spend` reads `no total daily cap set (CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD); provider caps are listed per provider (CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD, SAFE-14)`; none of these lines fails doctor. The owner's `/status` shows the total line (or `Spend cap (total): off (CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD); per-provider caps below`) and then `Spend (24h) on <id>: $<spent> of $<cap> daily cap (<n>%)`, with ` — 🛑 cap reached, calls to <id> stop and ask` or ` — ⚠️ past 80%`; anyone else sees only `Spend: Work is paused for budget.` while any cap is reached, never which one. The daemon's `spend.warning` line names `provider:<id>` for a provider cap, and `corvidinho --help` and `.env.example` list the new variable. SAFE-15 is only partly met: a cap stop is not yet an Approve card (that is the later spend-card work), so raising or removing the cap and restarting is still the only way past it. Known limits: a WATCH run's cap stop still reaches nobody but its `Work is paused for budget.` GitHub comment; doctor and `/status` flag an unpriced model only for each tier's first entry (an unpriced fallback entry under a cap still stops at call time); while a spend DM waits, the owner's `/status` note follows the last provider line; when one run crosses 80% of several caps, its `task run` result and the daemon's `spend.warning` log carry only the last of those warnings (with the shared DB the bridge's outbox still DMs every cap's warning).
- **A schedule the owner created now runs as the owner — with the owner's allowlisted tools and the must-ask Approve cards, but still never the shell, the runners or any Fledge command — and a card the owner denies or lets lapse ends the run with a blocking question; schedules anyone else created stay read-only** — [#330](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/330) (DISCORD-SCHEDULE-1.a, SAFE-3.a, AUTONOMY-6.a, AUTONOMY-9, AUTONOMY-10, SAFE-20, SAFE-12, SAFE-13, IDENTITY-11, IDENTITY-12; REQ-discord-741, REQ-agent-741, REQ-cli-741, REQ-plugins-065, REQ-discord-713, REQ-plugins-101, REQ-discord-476): every schedule ran with `actingIsAdmin: false`, so the owner's own schedules were community (read-only) like everyone else's. At each run, after the creator and channel gate, the scheduler now reads the owner as configured at that moment (the bridge re-reads its owner config from the same allowlist file and env it started with, `corvidinho daemon` re-reads `loadOwnerConfig`; a read that throws is logged `[scheduler] owner failed: <error>` and counts as no owner), and only a schedule whose creator is that live owner, not muted or deny-listed, gets the owner stamp; the tool layer still re-checks ADMIN at every call. Such a run gets the owner's allowlisted tools (the tier still filters) and the must-ask Approve cards, but the SAFE-3.a gate still refuses the `schedule` surface (no shell, runners or Fledge core runs; with those allowlisted each such run carries `[operator] SAFE-3.a: … allowlisted but not offered: scheduled runs never get them`) and a scheduled run, even the owner's, never discovers `fledge-<command>` plugin commands. Every other schedule stays community, and a team stamp in a scheduled run resolves to community in the tool layer, so no schedule is ever team. In a scheduled run a must-ask call the owner denied, let lapse (SAFE-20) or had already denied ends the run: `[operator] DISCORD-SCHEDULE-1.a: <tool> was refused on its Approve card; this scheduled run stops and asks`, then a stuck question — ``I didn't run `<tool>` (<the gate's scrubbed reason, at most 300 characters>), which needs the owner's OK (<rule>): the owner denied it on Approve card <id>, so nothing was done. This schedule waits until this is answered: how should it go on?`` (or `the owner already denied this exact call on Approve card <id>`, or `nobody answered Approve card <id> in time (SAFE-20: no answer means no)`) — so the run ends `blocked` with verify skipped, the owner is pinged, and the #322 blocking-ask rules hold the schedule (due runs skipped with the one wait note) until the owner or the creator answers or cancels it, instead of raising a new card every tick; outside schedules the refusal still goes back to the model. The SAFE-12 fence, the SAFE-13 scan and the answered-question hand-over use the same live owner. The owner's own schedule now gets project memory (REQ-plugins-101), while private memory reads (MEMORY-7.a) and `discord-send-file` stay refused because a schedule has no conversation channel. The schedule's own posts to its configured channel (result, question, wait note) still go out without a card; a `discord-post-message` the model starts inside the run asks (AUTONOMY-10). Owner schedules on a non-git project keep their separate `scoped-talk-schedule_*` folder (Leif, round 13). The DISCORD-SCHEDULE-3 / 3.a gates, the must-ask gate, the card engine and the schedule-question delivery are unchanged; no schema, env var or config key. Known limits: the creator gate and the question pings still use the owner loaded at start, so restart the bridge and the daemon after changing the owner; a must-ask card holds the schedule run, and one of the scheduler's 2 concurrent slots, for up to its 5-minute lifetime; the question posted to the schedule's channel quotes the gate's reason (for example a command word such as `kubectl`), scrubbed and without host paths; stopping a schedule run from Discord is not built yet (#332's `stop` / `cancel` reach Discord sessions only); an owner schedule run that leaves a diff now runs the verify lane (the project's own checks) in its schedule worktree, so test code the model wrote runs there even though the model itself gets no shell, and in a SpecSync repo the change it opens stays open for a human because the approve and finalize gate refuses schedules.

### Fixes

- **A busy shared DB no longer drops SAFE-5 audit rows or fails a process's DB open while other processes keep committing: both now retry the lock every millisecond up to the usual 5-second wait** — [#323](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/323) (SAFE-5; REQ-plugins-287, REQ-discord-287): a dangerous plugin run opens the shared DB, appends its audit row and closes, and both steps waited for the lock under SQLite's own busy handler, which backs off to one try every 100 ms once it has waited 228 ms. With other processes committing back to back (a commit holds the file for its whole sync, and a writer takes the lock again well under a millisecond after), a waiting process could miss every free moment for the whole 5 s `busy_timeout` and fail with `database is locked`, losing that SAFE-5 row (seen as a flaky CI test on slow disks, reproduced by slowing `fsync`). A new `retryWhileBusy` sets `busy_timeout` to 0 while it runs one statement or a `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, tries again every 1 ms on `SQLITE_BUSY` until the connection's own `busy_timeout` has passed, and restores it. `openCorvidinhoDb` now sets `foreign_keys` first (inside a transaction that pragma is a no-op), takes the write lock up front with a retried `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, and runs the migration check, the SAFE-6 re-scrub check and the `COMMIT` in that one transaction, so an open needs one free moment instead of one per statement, and processes that open a new DB file or one with a re-scrub due at the same time still take turns and all get in, as they did on 0.0.36; an open with nothing to migrate holds the lock for well under a millisecond and writes nothing. `appendAudit` retries only its `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (inside a caller's transaction it is still a savepoint), and its body and commit keep the normal `busy_timeout`. Every wait is still capped by `busy_timeout` (5000 ms); no new pragma, env var or config key, journal mode unchanged, other writers keep SQLite's handler, and opening `:memory:` is unchanged.
- **The SAFE-3.a approved prod-command test runs a stand-in `kubectl` first on PATH, never the host's real one; no product change** — [#333](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/333) (SAFE-3.a, AUTONOMY-9; REQ-agent-503): #324's owner-shell test sends `kubectl get pods; touch ran.marker` through the real `shell-exec`, so on CI runners, which ship a real `kubectl`, it spent 2.4 to 3.1 s running the runner's own `kubectl` and once hit bun's 5 s test timeout (bun then killed the still-running kubectl child as a dangling process), and on a machine with `KUBECONFIG` set a `bun test` from the verify lane would have run it against a real cluster (`KUBECONFIG` is not on the verify lane's drop list). `tests/agent.safe3a-owner-shell.test.ts` now puts a recording stand-in `kubectl` first on PATH (restored after each test); the approved case asserts exactly one call (`get pods`) in the talk worktree and none in the main checkout, and the denied case asserts none. REQ-agent-503 gains that acceptance criterion; its requirement text, product defaults, env names and code paths are unchanged.

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.37** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update: they read the environment and allowlist at start and spawn `task run` from the updated checkout, and the bridge brings the per-session run queue and stop (#332), the scheduler's live owner reader (#330), the fallback-aware spawn client and footer (#325), the `CORVIDINHO_ACTING_SURFACE` stamp without which no run is offered the SAFE-3.a tools (#324) and the per-cap spend DMs and pings (#328); `corvidinho daemon` brings its live owner reader and `llm.fallback` event, and `github watch` its `watch` stamp and `[watch] llm.fallback` line. No slash command is added or changed (still nine top-level commands), and the NDJSON protocol stays 2 (the new result and `usage` fields are optional). **Schema stays v15** (`SCHEMA_VERSION` 15, `SCRUB_RULES_VERSION` 3): this release has no forward-only migration step and records nothing new in `schema_meta`. Two module-owned additions happen on first use by a 0.0.37 process instead (a spend check, doctor or `/status` with a cap set, or the bridge's spend-DM and cap-ping passes), both idempotent and neither a schema bump (#328): `spend_alerts` gains `scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'total'` by `ALTER TABLE` (rows already there read as the total cap's; two processes racing to add it both carry on, and a column that is really missing still fails closed, stopping the call), and `spend_ledger` gains the index `idx_spend_ledger_provider_ts` on `(provider, ts)`. `spend_alerts.scope` joins the SAFE-6 re-scrub targets without a rules-version bump (a re-scrub skips a listed column an older table lacks). #323 changes how every process opens the shared DB (one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, retried every millisecond up to the 5 s `busy_timeout`, around the migration and re-scrub checks) with no schema, pragma or journal-mode change. Forget is unchanged from 0.0.36: it takes Approve on the owner's DM card plus the one-time code. v0.0.37 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main; the bridge's restart post links `…/releases/tag/v0.0.37`, which 404s until release.yml has created that Release.
- New and changed operator surface, read from the environment the bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` start with (restart them after changing it; see `.env.example`): new `CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD` (#328; optional, unset or blank = no provider caps): comma-separated `provider=USD`; each key is the endpoint host a configured model entry calls (with its port when not the scheme's default; `api.openai.com`, `api.anthropic.com`, `127.0.0.1:11434` for the default Ollama host; the `/status` LLM line and doctor show `<model> @ <host>`), matched case-insensitively; each amount is a plain USD figure up to 1e9 (`0` stops every call to that provider); a malformed entry, a duplicate key or a key no configured entry uses makes the whole setting invalid and stops every call until it is fixed. `CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD` is unchanged and is now called the total cap; either can be set without the other. `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` and `_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` keep their form, but every entry after the first is now a live fallback (#325): list only models you are willing to pay for, in order (e.g. `openai:gpt-4.1,anthropic:claude-sonnet-5,ollama:qwen3:30b`). `CORVIDINHO_ACTING_SURFACE` (#324) is a new internal stamp that the spawning client always overwrites — never set it yourself. `CORVIDINHO_LLM_TIER=code` in the bridge's environment is what puts the SAFE-3.a tools in the owner's catalog (the bridge passes no `--tier`, a local `task run --tier code` is refused by the gate, and the setting moves every run the bridge spawns to the code tier and its model). Allowlist entries that now mean more: `shell-exec`, `node-exec`, `python-exec`, `cargo-exec`, `fledge-lanes-run` and `fledge-run` reach the owner's model under the #324 conditions; `specsync-change-approve` and `specsync-change-finalize` let the run approve and archive its own change on Corvidinho (never offered to the model); `specsync-change-new`, `-answer` and `-status` are not dangerous and need no entry. No new allowlist-file key, slash command, CLI command or flag. Doctor gains one `spend provider:<id>` line per provider cap. Fixed constants with no knob: failover with no retry and no backoff, the 10-minute per-request timeout counting as a model failure; the DB lock retried every 1 ms up to `busy_timeout` 5000 ms; the SpecSync gate naming up to 5 paths; a schedule's must-ask question quoting at most 300 characters of the gate's reason; each cap warning at 80% and re-arming under 70%; the tool-surface budget raised from 8000 to 9000 tokens (#329). New log and operator lines: `[discord] llm.fallback: … (session <id>)`, `[watch] llm.fallback: … (session <id>)` and the daemon's `llm.fallback` warn event (#325); `[discord] run <run id> of session <session id> stopped by <user id>` (#332); `[scheduler] owner failed: <error>` (#330); in `task run` output `[operator] <a> failed (<reason>); falling back to <b>`, `[operator] SAFE-3.a: <tools> allowlisted but not offered: <why>`, `[operator] DISCORD-SCHEDULE-1.a: <tool> was refused on its Approve card; this scheduled run stops and asks`, `Repo ways (AGENT-18): …`, `SpecSync gate: …` and `SpecSync: …` (#325, #324, #330, #329); the daemon's `spend.warning` naming `provider:<id>` (#328).
- Needs operator action: (1) Fledge runs (#324): operator `corvidinho plugins run fledge-lanes-run` / `fledge-run` now start without GitHub or git credentials, so a project lane or task that pushes, opens a PR or needs the owner's git credential helper, ssh agent or gh login now fails there; move pushes, PRs and merges to the checked GitHub tools (the verify gate's own lane run is unchanged). (2) To let the owner's model use the shell, runners or Fledge runs, allowlist them and set `CORVIDINHO_LLM_TIER=code` in the bridge's environment (the bridge passes no `--tier`, so every run it spawns, for every role and its schedules, then runs at code tier and calls `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_CODE` when that is set); they are offered only in the owner's own chat, `/session start`, `/work` and their ask answers inside that talk's worktree, and a prod command still needs Approve plus the one-time code; keep the allowlist file and other secrets outside every talk worktree — the runners' own code and a Fledge lane or task still run as the bot's own user and can read or write files outside the worktree (Corvidinho's data dir included), which no check sees. (3) SpecSync repos (#329): in a project whose `.specsync/sdd.json` requires a change for meaningful files — Corvidinho's own checkout included — every run that changes such a path now needs `specsync` on PATH to open a change (without it `specsync-change-new` fails `specsync not on PATH (SPECSYNC-6: local binary required)`, the run cannot be verified and ends `failed` after its retries with the stuck question, and `/work` refuses its PR with `sdd-uncovered`), and a hi repo needs `hi` on PATH for `acceptance_criteria` answers; for the run to approve and archive its own change on Corvidinho (only in the checkout Corvidinho itself runs from or one of its worktrees, with origin github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho — a separate clone counts as another repo — and only in owner or team runs, never community, WATCH, schedules or workers), allowlist `specsync-change-approve` and `specsync-change-finalize` (without them the change stays open for a human). (4) Schedules (#330): every schedule the owner created runs as the owner from its next run — at code tier, with allowlisted `git-commit` / `git-push` / `github-pr-create` it can commit, push its `talk/schedule_*` branch and open a PR with no card (a push to a default or deploy branch still asks), and with the AUTONOMOUS-1 gate on at code tier it gets `delegate` / `council` (the workers stay community); review them and `/schedule pause` or `/schedule delete` any that should stay read-only. A schedule created by someone who is no longer the owner runs read-only, fenced and scanned as its creator's current role. The creator gate and the owner's pings still use the owner read at start, so restart the bridge and `corvidinho daemon` after changing the owner. (5) Provider caps (#328): set keys to the endpoint hosts your configured entries call, then restart; a key for a provider no entry uses stops every call.
- Other behaviour operators will notice: a second message in a Discord session waits for the run in progress instead of starting a parallel one, `stop` or `cancel` (the whole message) from the requester, or the owner replying to the progress message, ends it with `⏹ Stopping the run.` and `⏹ Stopped`, and a `cancel` message while a run is going no longer clears open questions; stopping or restarting the bridge now stops every Discord run still going at once (its process tree killed, nothing posted) and starts none of the waiting messages — their replies get the interrupted notice at the next start, and a stopped `/work` stays `running` until restart recovery fails it (#332); answers can end with `(model fallback: …)` visible to everyone, including community replies and public WATCH comments, a rate limit (429) or any other HTTP error moves the run to the next model at once, possibly a pricier one (the caps still price and stop every call), and the Discord footer names the answering model as its entry label, so non-`openai` models now show their `kind:` prefix (#325); the total cap's question gains `Stopped at cap: total.`, and non-owners see `Spend: Work is paused for budget.` in `/status` while any cap is reached (#328); every run in a repo with SpecSync changes, hi criteria (`hi/`) or a `.trust.toml` announces `Repo ways (AGENT-18): …` once at planning, whether or not it edits anything; runs that edit a SpecSync repo open and answer a change, and on Corvidinho, with `specsync-change-approve` and `specsync-change-finalize` allowlisted, spend several extra minutes approving, archiving and re-verifying it, while the file tools refuse `.specsync/changes/<id>/*.json` in every repo (#329); owner schedules can raise Approve cards (a card holds the run, and one of the scheduler's 2 concurrent slots, for up to 5 minutes while it waits) and stop with a blocking question when one is denied, lapses or was already denied (#330); fewer `database is locked` failures on a busy data dir (#323).
- Rollback to 0.0.36: the schema is v15 on both, so 0.0.36 opens the DB as it is and its backup and `backup restore` version check does not trip on a 0.0.37 snapshot. It ignores `spend_alerts.scope` (its inserts fill `total`) and reads per-provider warning, ping and re-arm rows as total-cap rows of the same cap value, so an undelivered provider warning can be DMed as a total-cap warning; it ignores `CORVIDINHO_PROVIDER_SPEND_CAPS_USD` (a box with only provider caps runs with no cap at all) and the extra index. It calls only each tier's first model again (no fallback, note, footer label or `llm.fallback` line), keeps the six SAFE-3.a tools out of every model catalog and gives Fledge core runs the owner's git credential helper, ssh agent and gh login again, runs every schedule read-only, drops the SpecSync ways, gate, change tools, `/work` `sdd-uncovered` check and record refusal, runs a second message in a session in parallel with the first and treats `stop` / `cancel` as before (`cancel` clears open questions, `stop` is a message), and its DB open and audit append can again lose to back-to-back writers.

## 0.0.36

### Real dev teammate (M3)

- **A repeated failing call is steered to change approach and then stops to ask, and a stuck GitHub run now reaches the owner as a Discord DM** — [#313](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/313) (AGENT-16, AGENT-16.a, AUTONOMY-2, AUTONOMY-4; REQ-agent-086, REQ-watch-086, REQ-discord-086): the tool loop ran an identical failing call as often as the model sent it, and a WATCH run that ended stuck only left `Needs your input: …` in its run summary comment (assignments and review requests post none), so the owner heard nothing on Discord. Every `task run` (chat, button and Answer-form resumes, `/session start`, `/work`, schedules, WATCH, `delegate` / `council` workers, the CLI) now keeps one repeat-failure guard per run, across its verify retries: a call is the tool name plus its canonical argv, and every `ok: false` result counts, refusals and denials included. A real change resets every count — a result that reports `filesChanged` (a failed one too), a successful write tool (`files-write` / `-edit` / `-delete`, `git-branch-create`, `git-commit`, `git-push`, the GitHub and Discord writers, `memory-forget` / `-override`, `delegate`, the shell and runners, `fledge-run`) or a successful Fledge plugin command; `web-fetch`, `danger-ping`, `fledge-lanes-run` and `council` are not changes — and a call's own success resets its own count. The 2nd identical failure gets, after its whole tool result and outside any SAFE-12 fence, `[Corvidinho harness — AGENT-16] This exact <tool> call has now failed <n> times with nothing changed in between (last error: "<scrubbed, at most 200 characters>"). Don't repeat it: change approach (a different tool or different arguments) or call ask-human. Making the same call again stops the run and asks the owner.` (a `delegate` / `council` result fenced for an injection hit quotes none of its error: `its error is in the untrusted result above`). When the model sends that call again after it has seen the steer, the call does not run (`not run: this exact call kept failing with nothing changed, even after the steer; stopped to ask (AGENT-16)`, plus an `[operator] AGENT-16: <tool> repeated after the steer with nothing changed; stopped to ask (last error: …)` note) and the attempt ends with the existing `stuck` ask `The same <tool> call keeps failing with nothing changed in between. How should I proceed?` (an offered tool's name or `(unknown tool)`, never error text), so `runTask` ends `blocked` without running verify and each surface's stuck path pings the owner (on a schedule it now also holds the schedule, #322); an identical call in the same `tool_calls` batch, or the first one in a fresh verify-retry conversation, gets the steer again, never the ask. The thresholds are constants: no env var, config key, flag, ask reason or NDJSON field. On WATCH, the spawn client now returns the result frame's validated `ask`, and after every finished run of any event type a `stuck` ask (a repeated failing call, or verification still failing after every retry; clarify asks stay with the commenter on GitHub) is recorded, SAFE-6 scrubbed, in the module-owned `watch_owner_asks` table — one row per issue or PR, a newer stuck ask replacing it, a later finished run on that thread that is not stuck dropping it — with one log line: `[watch] stuck ask <repo>#<n> id=<id>: queued for the owner's Discord ping (AGENT-16.a)`, `… the owner's Discord ping could not be sent — no Discord bridge is running on this data dir (CORVIDINHO_DATA_DIR); it is sent if one starts within a day; …` or `… could not be sent — no owner Discord id is configured (IDENTITY-3); …` (nothing stored). The bridge marks itself running in `schema_meta` (`discord_bridge_runner`, a `<pid>:<proc start>` id checked like schedule runners, cleared on stop) and on every scheduler tick (about 60 s) DMs the owner each pending ask — no mention, no channel, nobody else — as `GitHub <owner/repo>#<n> — answer on the thread: <url>` over the usual `⚠️ I'm stuck and need a human.` post with the quoted question; the DM has no buttons, and the owner answers on the GitHub thread. A DM that does not go out is handed back and retried after 10 minutes, an ask older than a day is dropped (`[discord] WATCH stuck ask <repo>#<n> id=<id>: gave up after a day without reaching the owner on Discord (AGENT-16.a)`), and a stopping bridge hands back a DM in flight. The WATCH summary comment is unchanged (no owner @mention) and nothing new is posted on GitHub.
- **You configure the models — `openai:`, `ollama:` or `anthropic:` entries, each kind with its own endpoint and key — and with none usable a run calls nothing and fails, while startup, `/status` and doctor say why** — [#320](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/320) (AGENT-13 in part, AGENT-10, AGENT-5, SAFE-6; REQ-agent-179, REQ-cli-079, REQ-discord-079, REQ-watch-079, REQ-agent-007, REQ-agent-015, REQ-agent-079, REQ-agent-085, REQ-agent-260, REQ-cli-003, REQ-cli-006, REQ-cli-007, REQ-cli-098, REQ-cli-262, REQ-cli-430, REQ-cli-505, REQ-discord-015): a tier with no `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL*` set used the built-in `gpt-4o-mini`, a box with no `CORVIDINHO_LLM_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` ran the demo stub (no model called; `/status` said `LLM: demo stub`), and only an OpenAI-compatible endpoint could be reached. `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` and the per-tier `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` (a tier's own key when it lists any entry, else `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL`) now take comma lists of `kind:model` entries; a bare name, or one whose prefix is not a kind (`qwen3:30b`), is OpenAI-compatible, and only the first entry of a tier is called (falling back to the next, AGENT-11, is later work). `openai` uses `CORVIDINHO_LLM_BASE_URL` (default `https://api.openai.com/v1`) with `CORVIDINHO_LLM_API_KEY`, else `OPENAI_API_KEY`; `ollama` uses `OLLAMA_HOST` (default `127.0.0.1:11434`, read the way Ollama reads it, `0.0.0.0` meaning loopback) plus `/v1`, with no key and no authorization header; `anthropic` uses `https://api.anthropic.com/v1` (Anthropic's OpenAI-compatible API, no endpoint override) with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. No kind sends another kind's key, and all three go through the same `chatCompletions` transport and SAFE-8 spend guard. The `gpt-4o-mini` default and the demo stub are gone. When a run's tier has no usable provider (no entry, or an `openai` / `anthropic` entry whose key is unset; a keyless `ollama:` entry counts), the run calls nothing and ends `failed` with the notice as its summary — with nothing set, `No model provider is configured: CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL is not set. Set CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL (or CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_READ / _TOOL / _CODE per tier) to openai:<model>, ollama:<model> or anthropic:<model>; there is no built-in default.`; with a missing key, `No model provider is configured: <entry> needs <key env>, which is not set.`; and startup, `/status` and doctor, which check all three tiers, say `No model provider is configured for some runs — <tier(s)> tier(s): <problem>; …` when only some tiers lack a usable provider or their problems differ (a run's own notice names only its own tier's problem) — naming env keys and models, never a key value. `task run` prints it as its first stderr line in text output and fails with it in every output mode; a WATCH run-summary comment carries it; Discord chat, button answers, `/session start`, `/work` and schedules post their usual `… failed (exit 1)` reply (the notice itself is not posted in the channel). At startup the bridge logs `[discord] No model provider is configured …`, `github watch` logs `[watch] No model provider is configured …` (not in a dry run), and `corvidinho daemon` logs an `llm.no_provider` warn line, its `daemon.started` line gaining an `llm` field (`<model> @ <host>` or `none`). The `/status` LLM line reads `LLM: <model> @ <host>` (non-`openai` kinds as `kind:model`) or `LLM: none — <notice>`: only the owner sees which tiers and which settings; anyone else sees only `No model provider is configured.` (or `… for some runs.`), and a caller that does not say who is asking gets that non-owner line, while the configured `<model> @ <host>` stays visible to everyone. `corvidinho doctor` and `init` print `[warn] llm` with the notice (never failing doctor), or `<key env> present (value not shown)` / `no key needed`, `model <entry> @ <host>` and, with per-tier keys set, each tier's entry as configured (`per tier: read …, tool …, code anthropic:…`). An unset model no longer counts as an unpriced model, so it never reads as paused for budget, and `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` joins the SAFE-6 secret env names scrubbed from error lines (it was already kept out of the verify lane and the shell). Not built: AGENT-13's headless agent CLI kind.
- **'Verified' now also needs the verify lane to show that tests ran and that none were deleted or turned off, and `/work` won't open a PR over a dropped test** — [#321](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/321) (AGENT-15, AGENT-14, AGENT-15.a; REQ-agent-185, REQ-discord-185, REQ-agent-002): since #308 the real git diff decides what changed, but any verify lane that exited 0 counted as verified — one with no test step, one where every test was skipped, or one after the run had deleted, retitled or `.skip`ped a test. After the lane (`fledge lanes run verify`) passes, a run is now verified only when (1) its output carries a test summary from a recognised runner — `bun test`, jest, vitest, `cargo test`, pytest or `go test` (colour escapes dropped; passed plus failed tests count, pytest xfailed / xpassed too, `go test` without `-v` one per `ok` package; skipped, todo and ignored never count) — showing at least one test ran, and (2) no test was dropped since the baseline: test files (JS/TS `*.test.*`, `*.spec.*`, `*_test.*`, `*_spec.*`, `__tests__/`; pytest `test_*.py` / `*_test.py`; Go `*_test.go`; Rust `.rs`) that differ from it anywhere under the repo root, not only the cwd, are compared by test name with the baseline commit's blobs (read-only `git ls-tree` / `git cat-file`), or with a file's start text when it was already dirty. A deleted or retitled test (in any state, an already `.skip` / `.todo` one included), a running test made conditional (`.if`, `.skipIf`, `.todoIf`, `.runIf`, pytest `skipif` / `skipUnless`) or off (`.skip`, `.todo`, `x`-prefixed, inside a skipped suite, silenced by an `.only` elsewhere in its file, a pytest `skip` mark, `#[ignore]`), and a conditional test turned off are drops; a renamed or moved file, or a test moved to another file, keeps its name and passes, and commented-out code is not a test (so commenting a test out drops it). Otherwise the attempt is a failed verify on the usual path (retried up to `[corvidinho] max_retries`, default 3, with the note first in the feedback, then `failed` with the stuck ask that pings the owner), with one `Verify gate: not verified: …` note naming every problem — `the verify lane (fledge lanes run verify) passed, but it printed no test summary Corvidinho recognises (bun test, jest, vitest, cargo test, pytest, go test), so nothing shows that tests ran; the verify lane needs a test step that prints one`, `the verify lane passed, but no test ran (…; skipped and todo tests don't count)`, `<n> test(s) were deleted or turned off (removed, retitled, skip, todo, or silenced by only): "name" (file), …; restore them (a moved test keeps its name)` (up to 10 named, then `and N more`) or `could not read the test files to check that no test was deleted` — and a verified run emits `Verify gate: <n> test(s) ran (bun test: <n>), and none were deleted.` Anything unreadable fails closed: a carried talk whose base branch is gone (which before this could still end `done`), a listing or blob git cannot read, a test file over 4 MiB, more than 2000 changed test files or more than 64 MiB of test source. A non-git project (or a start snapshot git cannot read) walks the cwd's test files at run start (no symlinks followed; dot-directories, `node_modules`, `target`, `vendor`, `dist`, `build`, `coverage`, `venv` and `__pycache__` skipped) and fails closed past 20,000 entries. In a talk worktree the carried merge-base baseline (AGENT-15.a) applies here too, so every later turn re-runs the lane until one ends verified. `/work`'s PR step now compares the tree about to be committed and pushed with the talk branch's merge-base (after the allowlist and repo gates and before the lane re-run, even when the run reported verified) and refuses with the new reason `tests-deleted` — `PR: not opened — <n> test(s) were deleted or turned off since the branch left <base> (removed, retitled, skip, todo, or silenced by only): "name" (file), …. The changes stay on branch <branch>.`, or `… could not check that no test was deleted since the branch left <base>. …` — with no plugin run and nothing pushed; its pre-push lane re-run must also show that tests ran, else `verify-failed` with the note. None of this can be turned off (AGENT-14), and `corvidinho doctor` does not check that a lane has a test step. Test names come from static parsing: a mis-parse fails closed (not verified, and the note may name a test that still exists), but aliased imports (`import { test as t }`), titles computed at run time and `t.Run` subtests, skips made at run time and tests turned off through runner config are not seen, so dropping or turning off such a test is not caught.

### Safe autonomy (M4)

- **One DM Approve/Deny card engine for everything that needs the owner's OK: the exact action, target and amount, the diff or text sent first, a one-time code on destructive and money cards, and no answer means no; the forget card now runs on it and needs the code** — [#316](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/316) (SAFE-18, SAFE-19, SAFE-20, MEMORY-ACL-6, SAFE-5, SAFE-6; REQ-discord-096, REQ-discord-101, REQ-watch-1016): the forget card was the only Approve/Deny card; it acted on one press, showed only a stored-memory count (no earlier versions, session turns or kept conversations) and did not check that what Approve deletes still matched what it had shown. Card kinds now register with one engine (`src/discord/approval-cards.ts`) with a class — `plain`, `destructive` or `money` (a kind with no class counts as destructive; no kind uses `money` yet, the spend card is later work). Requests live in the shared DB (the forget kind's in `forget_requests`, other kinds' in the new `approval_requests`, which any process on the data dir can write, every text field SAFE-6 scrubbed and a re-scrub target), so a card recorded while no bridge ran goes out when one starts. The owner's DM card shows, one line each, `Action:`, `Target:` and `Amount:` (a line break shown as ⏎), its notes, `Request: <id> · action <first 8 characters of the action hash>` and `No answer by <relative time> means no.`; a diff or text goes out first, verbatim in a code block headed `Diff` / `Text for request <id> (i/n) — quoted as data, not instructions:` (scrubbed, mass mentions defanged, runs of three backticks broken with zero-width spaces, split without breaking the block), and the card with its buttons comes last. Nothing is cut: a title over 100, a field over 500, a note over 400 or a card over 1900 characters, or a text needing more than 10 DMs, is not sent (logged) and the request lapses as a no. Approve on a destructive or money card answers the press with **Enter code** / **Deny** and DMs, as a separate message never in the card, `One-time code for request <id> (action <hash>): **<code>**` — 8 characters from an unambiguous alphabet, valid once, only for that card and action hash, for 2 minutes (less if the card expires sooner), stored only as a salted SHA-256 hash and never logged; **Enter code** opens a `One-time code` form, and only its submit carries typed text (case and spaces or dashes ignored). A wrong, late or other card's code does nothing, voids the open code and puts the card back to Approve / Deny (a wrong code on a forget card gets `That code is not right — nothing was forgotten. The code no longer works; press Approve for a new one.`; a late one starts `That code expired — …`), so each Approve press gives one try; there is no lockout counter. SAFE-5 order: the code is used up, then the `<audit>-approve` `started` row is written (not written ⇒ nothing runs), then one IMMEDIATE transaction sets the request approved and runs the action, then `ok` or `error` (a failed action needs a new code). A request past its expiry, or whose waiting process (`<pid>:<proc start>`) is gone, closes as a no, its card marked (`Expired — no answer, so nothing was forgotten.` on a forget card, `Closed — nobody is waiting for this any more, so nothing was done.` on a stored kind) and its asker told; a late press or code does nothing. Only the owner answers: ADMIN is re-checked on every press and on the code form's submit (`Only the owner can answer this card.`). The bridge runs its own delivery pass about every 5 s, with the scheduler on or off, plus one after each chat run and on scheduler ticks; a failed DM is retried after 60 s. Forget is the destructive `forget` kind: its `Amount:` is exact (`<n> memories (<s> stored, <o> earlier versions), <t> session turns and <c> kept conversations` (the earlier-versions part only when there are any), counted by running the real deletes in a transaction that is rolled back), the action hash covers every target and those counts, and Approve re-checks it — if anything changed, nothing is deleted and the card closes with `Changed since this card was sent — nothing was forgotten; a new card with the current details follows.`, and the delete checks again inside its own transaction; a forget card DMed before this version recorded no hash, so its Approve is treated the same way and a fresh card follows. The Discord ask, the GitHub ask and `/admin people forget` keep their texts, audit rows and 24 h lifetime; Approve now also needs the code, and the code step adds `approval-code-issue` / `approval-code-fail` audit rows. The gateway no longer cuts DMs, component replies, form replies or edits silently: a DM over 1900, or a component reply or update, form-submit reply (any form, the ask Answer form's included) or message edit over 2000 characters (the defang counted), is refused and logged (`[discord] <where>: <n> characters is over the <max>-character limit; not sent (never cut)`); channel posts and slash replies are still cut at 2000; answer parts are now defanged before they are split, and the private Choose message is cut visibly (`…`) at 1900, so existing callers never hit the refusal. Schema v14 (see Ops).
- **It asks the owner on an Approve card before any contact with prod or deploys (with the one-time code) and before every channel post it makes; anything else inside its guardrails it just does and says what it did** — [#319](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/319) (AUTONOMY-9, AUTONOMY-9.a, AUTONOMY-10 channel-post half, AUTONOMY-10.a channel-post half, AUTONOMY-11, SAFE-18, SAFE-19, SAFE-20; REQ-plugins-097, REQ-discord-097, REQ-agent-097, REQ-cli-097): nothing held a prod command, a push to a default branch or a channel post for the owner: `discord-post-message` posted as soon as its checks passed and `git-push` pushed `main` like any branch. `runPlugin` now has one must-ask gate, after the role gate and SAFE-1 and before SAFE-5 and the handler, for every caller (the tool loop on every surface, `/work`, schedules, WATCH, `delegate` / `council` workers and operator `corvidinho plugins run`). A command declares its class in code (`PluginCommand.mustAsk`, a class or a classifier over its args), so nothing a prompt says can change it, and a classifier that throws asks as prod. Prod asks raise a `mustask` card (destructive: Approve plus the #316 one-time code, AUTONOMY-9.a) titled `Prod / deploy — asks first (AUTONOMY-9) · from <surface>`, with `Action: <tool>: <why>`, the target and `Amount: 1 call (no money)`; channel posts raise a plain `mustask-post` card (one press) titled `Channel post — waits for your OK (AUTONOMY-10) · from <surface>`, with `Amount: 1 message (<n> characters)`; the exact text or command goes out first as quoted data (a `git-push` card has none; its target names the branch and remote). What asks: every `discord-post-message` post, dictated text and replies to the owner included (it shares its channel, requester-flag, token and strict-mode checks with the handler (`preparePost`), so a post they would refuse raises no card; the DISCORD-8 requester lookup runs only after Approve, and a dry run, `CORVIDINHO_DISCORD_DRY_RUN=1`, raises none); `git-push` of the remote's recorded default branch, or of `main`, `master`, `trunk`, `default`, `production`, `prod`, `live`, `release`, `stable`, `deploy` or `gh-pages` whatever default is recorded (other branches never ask); `shell-exec`, read with the SAFE-3 clamp's walker and through exec wrappers, on the ssh family or remote `rsync`, root, the box's services, packages, containers, firewall or cron, secrets tools, cloud, hosting, cluster, infrastructure and DNS CLIs, `gh` on secrets, variables, workflows, releases or `api` paths, `git push` and git aliases for it, `npx` / `bunx` / `bun x` / `pnpm dlx` of one, package scripts (pre / post and install lifecycle scripts included), `make` / `just` recipes, and inline interpreter code or in-root scripts that name one — read-only looks included (`systemctl status`, `docker ps`, `dig`, `terraform plan`), and anything it can't read asks (a missing script or recipe, `make -C` / `-f`, a package-manager option that picks another package.json or workspace, a command named by an expansion); the `node-exec` / `python-exec` / `cargo-exec` runners on the same table words in argv, inline code or an in-root script, plus SSH, cloud and secrets client libraries (`paramiko`, `ssh2`, `boto3`, `aws-sdk`, `hvac` …); `fledge-run` / `fledge-lanes-run` on the task and lane commands in `fledge.toml` and `.fledge/lanes/*.toml` (Corvidinho's own verify lane asks nothing); discovered `fledge-<command>` plugins on table words in their name or argv. Updating itself to a tagged release is exempt exactly as AUTONOMY-9 says: `CORVIDINHO_REF=v<X.Y.Z> <installed checkout>/scripts/corvidinho-update.sh` (or `bash` on it), nothing else typed, and a tag that checkout has; the talk worktree's own copy of the script asks. The held call waits in-process for up to 5 minutes (honouring the run's abort) and says so once — `[operator] AUTONOMY-9: waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card with the one-time code (<tool>: <why>; request <id>; no answer by <ISO time> means no).` (once approved it adds `[operator] AUTONOMY-9: the owner approved request <id>; running <tool>.`) as a `Text` frame in `task run` (no protocol bump) or on stderr elsewhere, and the Discord thinking status shows `⏳ waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card` (the only `Text` frame it shows). It runs only on an approval it consumes once; a deny, no answer, or a stopped run runs nothing and returns exit 2 (130 when stopped) with `refused (AUTONOMY-9): <tool>: <why> — the owner denied it on Approve card <id>, so nothing was done (SAFE-20). Don't send the same call again; tell the requester.`, `… — no answer on the owner's Approve card <id> in time, so nothing was done (SAFE-20: no answer means no). The running Discord bridge DMs the card to the owner; with no bridge running it lapses.` or `stopped (…): the run was interrupted while waiting for the owner's OK on Approve card <id>, so nothing was done.` (`AUTONOMY-10` for posts); a run stopped just as the owner approves runs nothing. The same denied call (tool, args, target and requester) is refused afterwards with no new card (`… the owner already denied this exact call on Approve card <id>, so no new card is raised …`), a `delegate` / `council` worker is refused without a card (`… Hand it back to the lead run, which asks.`), and with no owner configured the call is refused at once (IDENTITY-1). Notes and refusals are SAFE-6 scrubbed; a refused call is audited `denied` under its tool's name, and the cards write `mustask-*` / `mustask-post-*` rows. The tool-loop system prompt gains one AUTONOMY-11 sentence: act inside the guardrails and say what you did, never call `ask-human` for permission first, and never repeat a call the owner denied. Not gated: `discord-send-file` (it attaches in the conversation's own channel), a schedule's own result posts to its configured channel, and the AGENT-14 verify lane, which runs directly; the first-20 public-thread replies half of AUTONOMY-10 / 10.a is later work, and spend over a cap (AUTONOMY-8) stays with the SAFE-8 guard. `shell-exec`, the runners, `fledge-run` and `fledge-lanes-run` are still never offered to the model (SAFE-3.a is pending), so today those classes reach only operator `plugins run`; in a model run the gate meets only `discord-post-message`, `git-push` and discovered `fledge-<command>` plugins; prod is recognised by tool, not by host, so a `web-fetch` of your own prod site does not ask.
- **Only the owner sees spend amounts and cap settings: everyone else sees `💸 Work is paused for budget.`, and the 80% warning and a cap stop's details reach the owner by DM** — [#317](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/317) (SAFE-14.a, SAFE-8, DISCORD-15.a; REQ-discord-098, REQ-discord-215, REQ-discord-347, REQ-discord-734, REQ-discord-071, REQ-agent-098): a spend-cap post quoted the run's cap question (spend, the call's estimate, the cap and `CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD`) under `💸 I paused before spending more — the daily spend cap needs the operator.`, the 80% warning (`⚠️ Spend warning (SAFE-8): $… of the $… daily cap used in the last 24h (…%). …`) was added to the next channel post whoever the run was for, and `/status` showed everyone the 24 h spend line. Every post about a spend-cap stop — the chat answer, a button-pick resume, `/work`, `/session start`, a schedule's own ask post and the delivery pass that posts a daemon's ask — now says only `💸 Work is paused for budget.` (the owner still mentioned once per cap episode; no question, amount, cap value or setting name), and the same words replace the ask status (`💸 Work is paused for budget`, was `💸 Paused at the spend cap`), the `/work` PR line (`PR: not opened — Work is paused for budget.`), the slash owner notice (`💸 <@owner> /work …: Work is paused for budget.`) and the WATCH comment (`Work is paused for budget.`, was `Paused before calling the model: the operator's daily spend cap (SAFE-8) needs attention, so nothing more was spent.`). That text is the run summary of every spend-cap stop (`SPEND_CAP_SUMMARY`), so `task run` output and the daemon's `run.needs_human` log `message` now read `Work is paused for budget.` too; the ask question, which `task run` still prints, keeps the details. The bridge (new `src/discord/spend-dm.ts`, through the gateway's existing `sendDm`) DMs the owner the 80% warning — claimed from the `spend_alerts` outbox that any run on the data dir writes and rebuilt from its integer amounts, never added to a channel post, split part, collapsed edit, fallback reply, slash owner notice or schedule post, the owner's own runs included — and a cap stop's details under `💸 Work is paused for budget. Only you see these details (SAFE-14.a).` (`In <#channel>:` and the quoted, scrubbed and defanged question), once per cap episode alongside the channel ping; a schedule ask whose post keeps failing and is retried every tick sends them once per run. DMs go out after each chat, button-pick, `/work` and `/session start` run and on every scheduler tick; one that does not go out is kept (the warning handed back to `spend_alerts`, a stop's details held in memory, a newer stop replacing an older one) and retried on the next pass, logged once per failure streak and without amounts (`[discord] spend DM to the owner did not go out (SAFE-14.a) — kept and retried on the next scheduler tick; check that the owner accepts DMs from server members`). `/status` re-checks ADMIN: the owner's spend line is unchanged and adds `— ⚠️ a spend DM to you has not gone out yet (retried every tick)` while one waits; anyone else gets no spend line, or `Spend: Work is paused for budget.` while runs stop at the spend check (cap reached, an unpriced model, an invalid cap value or an unreadable ledger). DISCORD-15.a footers (tokens and cost only on the owner's own runs) are unchanged. No env var, config key, table, column or schema change.
- **A schedule run's question now stays open until the owner or the schedule's creator answers or cancels it on Discord, and the schedule's next runs wait, with one note, until then** — [#322](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/322) (AUTONOMY-6.a, AUTONOMY-6, AUTONOMY-5, SAFE-12, SAFE-12.a, SAFE-13, SAFE-14.a, DISCORD-ASK-4.a, DISCORD-ASK-5; REQ-discord-606, REQ-discord-045, REQ-discord-347, REQ-discord-353, REQ-discord-548): a schedule posted its run's question as text with no buttons and nothing could answer or cancel it, the schedule kept running at every slot, a schedule with no channel never posted its question, and a run that could not start was retried every slot until the 5-failure auto-pause. Every ask a schedule run records — clarify, stuck (AGENT-16's repeated call included) and spend-cap asks, a run that could not start (`project resolve failed` / `worktree failed`), the auto-pause and a SAFE-13 refusal (#307) — now stays open until the creator or the live owner answers or cancels it; only the ask on the schedule's newest finished run counts, and `/schedule resume` does not close it. While one is open, the bridge's and `corvidinho daemon`'s ticks skip each due run (`next_run_at` moves to the next slot with the same compare-and-set as a claim; no run row, counters unchanged, nothing made up later; listed in the tick's `skipped`), and the bridge posts one wait note per open ask after the ask itself — `⏸️ <schedule>: its next runs are waiting until its last question is answered or cancelled. Runs that come due meanwhile are skipped, not made up.` — with no mention, no amount and the ask's own buttons, so a question whose post was lost (a crash between claim and post, a deleted message) can still be closed. The ask post carries one row of buttons with the run id as the ask id (`cvask:open|pick|answer|cancel:srun_<id>`; `cancel` is a new kind): **Choose** + **Cancel** for listed choices (stored scrubbed in `ask_options`), **Answer** + **Cancel** for free text, and **Cancel** only for a spend-cap stop, whose post stays `💸 Work is paused for budget.` (#317). Its hint (`Press **Choose** to answer privately, or **Cancel** to drop the question. This schedule's next runs wait until then.`, or the **Answer** variant) never invites a reply, a channel reply does not answer, and the controls never lapse while the ask is open (DISCORD-ASK-5's ~30-minute expiry stays for session asks). A schedule with no channel sends its ask, controls and note to the live owner by DM (the #316 engine's `sendDm` path); with no owner configured nothing is sent, the ask stays pending and the schedule waits until an owner is set (restart the bridge; its next pass DMs the question) or `/schedule delete`. Presses are gated in order: the schedule's channel and the press channel still allowlisted (zero-width ack otherwise, the allowlist tip for an admin; for a channel-less schedule a DM only, with no channel check), the actor gate, mute and rate limits, then the ask open and the presser the creator or the live owner (`This choice isn’t for you (or it was already answered).`; other admins and team members cannot answer). A pick closes it `picked` (`Got it — **<label>**. The schedule's next run goes ahead with it.`; an option id it does not have gets `that choice expired`); the Answer form's text is SAFE-6 scrubbed and closes it `answered` (`Got it — the schedule's next run gets your answer.`), `cancel` typed there cancels, a thin answer is restated privately and leaves it open (AUTONOMY-5), and a non-owner's answer that trips the SAFE-13 detector closes nothing, pings the owner in the schedule's channel and writes one `injection-suspected` row; **Cancel** closes it `cancelled` (`Cancelled — the schedule's next runs go ahead without an answer.`); on a paused schedule each of those acks adds `The schedule is paused, so its next run waits for /schedule resume.` Nothing runs at a press: the next due slot runs as usual, and that run alone gets `[Prior question this schedule's last run asked (the human answered it, …): <question>]` and `Human answer:` after its prompt — the owner's answer as given, anyone else's fenced as their words (`ask-answer` / `ask-pick`); a cancelled ask hands nothing on. A run that could not start now blocks its schedule at that failure instead of being retried every slot (the failure still counts as one failed run toward the 5-in-a-row auto-pause), and every in-process ask post that does not go out is handed back for the next delivery pass. Schema v15 (see Ops).

### Spec accuracy

- **REQ-discord-212 now says exactly where a parent-channel deny reaches its threads, and tests pin every path; no behaviour change** — [#314](https://github.com/CorvidLabs/Corvidinho/pull/314) (DISCORD-5, DISCORD-DENY-1, ALLOW-3; REQ-discord-212, REQ-discord-311, REQ-discord-476): the spec said a thread counts as not allowlisted on every path when the thread or its parent is on `deny_channels`; with the thread allowlisted by its own id and only the parent deny-listed, the code refuses it only where the bridge knows the parent. The spec now states what the code does. A deny-listed thread is refused on every path (`discord-post-message` now named in that list). A deny on the parent alone refuses such a thread for messages in it (@mention, thread continuation and reply-to-bot, a talk started before the deny included), for the restart row, `discord-send-file` and forget-card fallback notice of the run a message starts or continues, and for the ask buttons of a session a message started in the thread and the runs they resume (`isMonitoredConversation`). Slash commands (autocomplete likewise), `/schedule create` and its ticks, `discord-post-message --channel`, and a `/session start` / `/work` session in the thread — its run and its ask-button picks with their files, restart rows and forget-card notice — gate only the id they are given, so they still serve that thread; a reply in the thread to such a session's answer is a message run and carries the parent. REQ-discord-311 (a restart row records the parent only for a message in a thread or a pick on a session a message started there; a row with no parent is recovered) and REQ-discord-476 (which runs pass `replyParentChannelId`) say the same, three stale duplicate lines were removed from `discord.spec.md`, and `docs/discord.md` ("Deny always wins", the send-file bullets) was corrected. `tests/discord.thread-deny.test.ts` goes from 18 to 29 tests across the three shapes (deny on the thread; thread listed and parent denied; both listed and parent denied) on each path, and they pass on the code as it was. No source change: to shut a thread on every path, deny-list the thread itself.

### Ops

- Package version **0.0.36** — restart the Discord bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` after update; no slash command is added or changed (still nine top-level commands). **Schema v13 → v15**: the first open of the shared DB after update, by any Corvidinho process, runs two forward-only, idempotent steps in order, each recording its version in `schema_meta` (`version`) once it has run. v13 → v14 (#316) adds `forget_requests.action_hash` and creates `approval_requests` (`id`, `kind`, `class`, `title`, `action`, `target`, `amount`, `text`, `text_label`, `action_hash`, `requester`, `waiter`, `status`, `created_at`, `expires_at`, `card_channel_id`, `card_message_id`, `card_posted_at`, `decided_at`, `decided_by`, `used_at`) with `idx_approval_requests_kind_status`, and `approval_codes` (`kind`, `request_id`, `action_hash`, `salt`, `code_hash` — never the code — and created / expires / used / voided times) with `idx_approval_codes_request`. v14 → v15 (#322) adds eight `schedule_runs` columns (`ask_options`, `ask_blocking` default 0, `ask_closed_at`, `ask_outcome`, `ask_answer`, `ask_closed_by`, `ask_skip_at`, `ask_note_at`) and the partial index `idx_schedule_runs_open_ask`, then makes an ask no bridge has posted yet on its schedule's newest finished run (a daemon's question waiting for a bridge, a handed-back auto-pause ask) blocking, so it is posted with its buttons and its schedule waits, and closes every other ask recorded before v15 as `superseded` (already posted without a Cancel, or moot), so no schedule starts out waiting on it and none is posted again. Later opens run neither step. `SCRUB_RULES_VERSION` stays 3, so there is no re-scrub pass; new SAFE-6 re-scrub targets are `approval_requests` (`title`, `action`, `target`, `amount`, `text`), `schedule_runs.ask_answer` and `ask_options` (JSON) and `watch_owner_asks.question`. #313's `watch_owner_asks` table is module-owned and created on first use (no version bump), and a running bridge keeps the `schema_meta` key `discord_bridge_runner`. A build with an older schema (0.0.35) opens the v15 DB without refusing it: its steps stop at v13, it leaves the stored version at 15 and ignores the tables and columns it does not know. A 0.0.35 daemon or bridge on the v15 DB records schedule asks with `ask_blocking = 0`, which do not hold their schedule and cannot be closed with the buttons, so restart the bridge and the daemon together. v0.0.36 gets its tag and Release from CI when this bump lands on main; the bridge's restart post links `…/releases/tag/v0.0.36`, which 404s until release.yml has created that Release.
- New and changed operator surface (#320), read from the environment the bridge, `corvidinho daemon` and `github watch` start with (restart them after changing it; see `.env.example` and `docs/DISCORD-GO-LIVE.md` E.9): `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` is now required (unless each of `_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` lists its own entry) and has no default — a comma list of `openai:<model>`, `ollama:<model>` or `anthropic:<model>` entries or bare OpenAI-compatible ids (a blank entry, or a kind with no model such as `ollama:`, is skipped; only the first entry is called); `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` (optional, same form, used for their tier when they list any entry, else `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL`); `CORVIDINHO_LLM_BASE_URL` (now only for `openai` entries; default `https://api.openai.com/v1`); `CORVIDINHO_LLM_API_KEY`, else `OPENAI_API_KEY` (only for `openai` entries); new `OLLAMA_HOST` (for `ollama` entries; default `127.0.0.1:11434`; a host, `host:port` or URL, port 11434 when a bare host gives none (a URL with a scheme and no port uses that scheme's port, as Ollama does), `0.0.0.0` / `[::]` read as loopback; no key is sent); `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (now the key for `anthropic` entries, endpoint fixed at `https://api.anthropic.com/v1`; scrubbed from error lines); `CORVIDINHO_LLM_TIER` (unchanged, default `tool`) picks the tier `/status`, doctor and the daemon's `llm` field describe. Removed: the built-in `gpt-4o-mini` default and the demo stub. No new allowlist-file key, slash command, CLI command or flag. New Discord controls: **Enter code** and the `One-time code` form on destructive cards (`cvok:<kind>:code|submit:<id>`), and schedule-question **Choose** / **Answer** / **Cancel** (`cvask:…:srun_<id>`, `cvask:cancel:…` new). Doctor and `init`: `[warn] llm` carries the no-provider notice (replacing `task run uses the demo stub`) and never fails doctor. Daemon JSON log: `llm.no_provider` (warn) and an `llm` field on `daemon.started`. Fixed constants with no knob: the AGENT-16 steer on the 2nd identical failure; one-time codes of 8 characters for 2 minutes; card limits (title 100, field 500, note 400, card 1900 characters, text in at most 10 DMs), over which a card is not sent and lapses; the card pass every 5 s and a failed card DM retried after 60 s; must-ask cards lapse after 5 minutes; stuck WATCH DMs retried after 10 minutes and dropped after a day; forget cards keep 24 h; the #321 limits (4 MiB per test file, 2000 changed test files, 64 MiB of test source, 20,000 walked entries). New log lines: `[discord] No model provider is configured …`, `[watch] No model provider is configured …`, `[watch] stuck ask <repo>#<n> id=<id>: …` (queued / could not be sent / recording it failed), `[discord] WATCH stuck ask <repo>#<n> id=<id>: owner DMed (AGENT-16.a)` and its `owner DM failed: …`, `the owner DM did not go out; retried in 10 min`, `gave up after a day …` and `hand-back failed: …` variants, `[discord] WATCH stuck ask delivery failed: …`, `[discord] bridge mark not cleared: …` (on stop), `[discord] approval card <kind> <id> not sent: …`, `[discord] approval card pass failed: …`, `[discord] <where>: <n> characters is over the <max>-character limit; not sent (never cut)`, `[discord] spend DM to the owner did not go out (SAFE-14.a) — …`, `[discord] spend DM to the owner waits: no DM path yet (SAFE-14.a) — …`, `[discord] spend DM pass failed (SAFE-14.a): …`, the other `[discord] approval card <kind> <id>: …` error lines (telling the asker failed, answering the press failed, after approve, could not void codes), the must-ask gate's `[operator] AUTONOMY-9: waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card …` (`AUTONOMY-10` for posts) and `[operator] AUTONOMY-9: the owner approved request <id>; running <tool>.` (on stderr from `corvidinho plugins run`, as `Text` frames in `task run`), and in `task run` output `[operator] AGENT-16: <tool> repeated after the steer with nothing changed; stopped to ask (last error: …)`.
- Models need operator action (#320): a box that set only `CORVIDINHO_LLM_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` must also set `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` (`CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL=gpt-4o-mini` keeps the old model), a box that set only some of `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL_READ` / `_TOOL` / `_CODE` needs `CORVIDINHO_LLM_MODEL` too (the other tiers no longer fall back to `gpt-4o-mini`, so their runs, read-tier `delegate` / `council` workers included, fail until it is set), and a box with no key, which ran the demo stub, now needs a real entry (for example `ollama:<model>` with `OLLAMA_HOST`). Until then every run on every surface fails (Discord replies `… failed (exit 1)`, WATCH run summary comments carry the notice, and a schedule auto-pauses after 5 such runs and then waits for `/schedule resume` plus an answer or **Cancel** on its pause question) and the bridge, `github watch` and the daemon log the notice at start; doctor's `[warn] llm` does not fail doctor, so the updater's doctor step does not roll back — set the model wherever the bridge, daemon and `github watch` get their environment before or right after updating. With `CORVIDINHO_DAILY_SPEND_CAP_USD` set, an `ollama:` model, or a model id the price table does not list, has no known price, so every call on that tier stops and asks at the spend check (SAFE-8, SAFE-16) and non-owners see `Spend: Work is paused for budget.`; unset the cap to use such a model. `/status` shows the configured `<model> @ <host>` to everyone, so a LAN `OLLAMA_HOST` is visible to non-owners.
- Verify needs a test summary (#321): each project's verify lane (`fledge.toml` `[lanes.verify]` or a `.fledge/lanes/*.toml` import) must run its tests with `bun test`, jest, vitest, `cargo test`, pytest or `go test` and print that runner's usual summary; a lane with no test step, or tests on another runner (`node --test`, mocha, rspec, `swift test` …), now makes every run that changes something end `failed` after its `max_retries` model retries (each one paid for, and the model cannot edit the lane, which SAFE-2 / SAFE-2.a protect) with a stuck question pinging the owner, and `/work` no longer opens its PR; `corvidinho doctor` does not warn about it. Removing an obsolete test, even an already skipped one, now waits for a human; a talk whose last run did not end verified and whose base branch was deleted cannot end verified until the branch is back; a non-git project of more than 20,000 entries (outside the skipped directories) can never be verified. Corvidinho's own lane prints a `bun test` summary.
- Approval cards and the must-ask gate (#316, #319) need a running bridge on the same `CORVIDINHO_DATA_DIR` (the card is raised in the shared DB), an owner Discord id (`CORVIDINHO_OWNER_DISCORD_ID` or `[owner]`) and an owner who accepts DMs from server members; a card whose DM fails is retried every minute until it lapses as a no. Approving a forget request now takes Approve, then the one-time code the bridge DMs, typed into **Enter code** within 2 minutes; a forget card a 0.0.35 bridge DMed and nobody answered is replaced by a fresh card on its first Approve. Every `discord-post-message` post the model makes now waits for the owner's press, and `git-push` of the remote's recorded default branch or a `main`-like branch (main, master, trunk, production, release, gh-pages …) and prod commands wait for Approve plus the code; operator `corvidinho plugins run discord-post-message …`, `git-push` of such a branch and prod `shell-exec` / runner / Fledge commands also wait up to 5 minutes for the DM card (there is no terminal prompt; stderr says `[operator] AUTONOMY-<n>: waiting for the owner's OK on an Approve card … (…; request <id>; no answer by <time> means no).`), refuse with exit 2 when it lapses (no bridge running) and are refused at once with no owner configured, so a CLI-only box, or one with no `[owner]`, can no longer post to a channel or push a default branch through Corvidinho. The self-update command `CORVIDINHO_REF=v<X.Y.Z> <installed checkout>/scripts/corvidinho-update.sh` is exempt. A denied call stays denied for good for the same tool, arguments, target and requester; change the call to ask again.
